########## CONFIG: credit.ts ##########
// src/game/config/credit.ts

export interface CreditRateBand {
  maxRating: number;
  monthlyRate: number;
  limitCoefficient: number;
}

export const CREDIT = {
  startRating: 100,
  maxRating: 1000,
  minRating: 0,

  /** РџРѕР»РѕСЃС‹ СЃС‚Р°РІРѕРє РїРѕ СЂРµР№С‚РёРЅРіСѓ */
  bands: [
    { maxRating: 300,  monthlyRate: 0.03, limitCoefficient: 2 }, // 36% РіРѕРґРѕРІС‹С…
    { maxRating: 700,  monthlyRate: 0.02, limitCoefficient: 3 }, // 24% РіРѕРґРѕРІС‹С…
    { maxRating: 1000, monthlyRate: 0.01, limitCoefficient: 5 }, // 12% РіРѕРґРѕРІС‹С…
  ] as CreditRateBand[],

  /** РЎСЂРѕРєРё РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Р° РІ РёРіСЂРѕРІС‹С… РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°С… */
  terms: [3, 6, 12] as const,

  /** РњР°РєСЃРёРјСѓРј Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… РєСЂРµРґРёС‚РѕРІ */
  maxActiveLoans: 1,

  /** РЁС‚СЂР°С„ Р·Р° РїСЂРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РєСѓ вЂ” % Рє РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РєСѓ РґРѕР»РіР° */
  latePenalty: 0.10,

  /** РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ СЂРµР№С‚РёРЅРіР° */
  ratingChanges: {
    paymentOnTime: 50,
    earlyPayoff: 75,
    fullPayoff: 100,
    missPayment: -150,
    default: 0,
  },
} as const;

export function creditBandFor(rating: number): CreditRateBand {
  for (const b of CREDIT.bands) {
    if (rating <= b.maxRating) return b;
  }
  return CREDIT.bands[CREDIT.bands.length - 1];
}

/** Р›РёРјРёС‚ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Р° = netMonthlyProfit Г— limitCoefficient. РќРёРєР°РєРёС… РґРѕРї. Г—3. */
export function creditLimitFor(netMonthlyProfit: number, rating: number): number {
  const band = creditBandFor(rating);
  return Math.max(0, netMonthlyProfit * band.limitCoefficient);
}



########## ECONOMY: ledger.ts ##########
import type { Money } from '../domain/types';
import {
  moneyAdd,
  moneyCmp,
  moneySub,
  ZERO_MONEY,
} from '../domain/money';

export type LedgerDirection = 'income' | 'expense';

export interface LedgerEntry {
  id: string;
  monthIndex: number;
  accountId: string;

  direction: LedgerDirection;
  category: string;

  amount: Money;
  description: string;

  relatedEntityId?: string;
}

export interface WalletTransactionInput {
  balance: Money;
  amount: Money;
  direction: LedgerDirection;

  monthIndex: number;
  accountId: string;
  category: string;
  description: string;

  allowNegative?: boolean;
  relatedEntityId?: string;
}

export type WalletTransactionResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      balance: Money;
      entry: LedgerEntry;
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      balance: Money;
      reason: string;
    };

function createId(): string {
  if (
    typeof crypto !== 'undefined' &&
    typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function'
  ) {
    return crypto.randomUUID();
  }

  return `ledger-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
    .toString(36)
    .slice(2)}`;
}

export function applyWalletTransaction(
  input: WalletTransactionInput,
): WalletTransactionResult {
  if (moneyCmp(input.amount, ZERO_MONEY) < 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      balance: input.balance,
      reason: 'РЎСѓРјРјР° РѕРїРµСЂР°С†РёРё РЅРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РѕС‚СЂРёС†Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕР№.',
    };
  }

  if (
    input.direction === 'expense' &&
    !input.allowNegative &&
    moneyCmp(input.balance, input.amount) < 0
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      balance: input.balance,
      reason: 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІ.',
    };
  }

  const nextBalance =
    input.direction === 'income'
      ? moneyAdd(input.balance, input.amount)
      : moneySub(input.balance, input.amount);

  const entry: LedgerEntry = {
    id: createId(),
    monthIndex: input.monthIndex,
    accountId: input.accountId,
    direction: input.direction,
    category: input.category,
    amount: input.amount,
    description: input.description,
    relatedEntityId: input.relatedEntityId,
  };

  return {
    ok: true,
    balance: nextBalance,
    entry,
  };
}

export function sumLedgerCategory(
  entries: LedgerEntry[],
  category: string,
  direction?: LedgerDirection,
): Money {
  return entries
    .filter(
      (entry) =>
        entry.category === category &&
        (!direction || entry.direction === direction),
    )
    .reduce(
      (total, entry) => moneyAdd(total, entry.amount),
      ZERO_MONEY,
    );
}



########## ECONOMY: capital.ts ##########
import type {
  Money,
} from '../domain/types';
import {
  moneyAdd,
  moneyMul,
  moneySub,
  moneySum,
} from '../domain/money';

export interface CapitalInput {
  cash: Money;

  businessValues: Money[];
  fundValues: Money[];
  bondValues: Money[];
  metalValues: Money[];

  stockPositions: Array<{
    shares: number;
    currentPrice: Money;
  }>;

  realEstateValues: Money[];
  transportValues: Money[];

  liabilities: Money[];
}

export interface CapitalBreakdown {
  cash: Money;
  businesses: Money;
  funds: Money;
  bonds: Money;
  metals: Money;
  stocks: Money;
  realEstate: Money;
  transport: Money;
  liabilities: Money;
  netWorth: Money;
}

export function calculateCapital(
  input: CapitalInput,
): CapitalBreakdown {
  const businesses = moneySum(input.businessValues);
  const funds = moneySum(input.fundValues);
  const bonds = moneySum(input.bondValues);
  const metals = moneySum(input.metalValues);
  const realEstate = moneySum(input.realEstateValues);
  const transport = moneySum(input.transportValues);

  const stocks = input.stockPositions.reduce(
    (total, position) =>
      moneyAdd(
        total,
        moneyMul(position.currentPrice, position.shares),
      ),
    '0',
  );

  const liabilities = moneySum(input.liabilities);

  const assets = [
    input.cash,
    businesses,
    funds,
    bonds,
    metals,
    stocks,
    realEstate,
    transport,
  ].reduce((total, amount) => moneyAdd(total, amount), '0');

  return {
    cash: input.cash,
    businesses,
    funds,
    bonds,
    metals,
    stocks,
    realEstate,
    transport,
    liabilities,
    netWorth: moneySub(assets, liabilities),
  };
}




########## LOGISTICS: inventory.ts ##########
import type {
  InventoryItem,
  Money,
  ResourceDefinition,
  ResourceRequirement,
  Warehouse,
} from '../domain/types';
import {
  moneyAdd,
  moneyCmp,
  moneyDiv,
  moneyMul,
  moneySub,
  ZERO_MONEY,
} from '../domain/money';

export type InventoryResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      warehouse: Warehouse;
      balance: Money;
      spent: Money;
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      reason: string;
      warehouse: Warehouse;
      balance: Money;
    };

export interface ResourceConsumptionResult {
  warehouse: Warehouse;
  supplied: boolean;
  missingResources: Array<{
    resourceId: string;
    required: number;
    available: number;
  }>;
  consumedValue: Money;
}

export function createWarehouse(capacity = 100): Warehouse {
  return {
    capacity,
    items: {},
  };
}

export function getWarehouseUsedCapacity(
  warehouse: Warehouse,
): number {
  return Object.values(warehouse.items).reduce(
    (total, item) => total + item.quantity,
    0,
  );
}

export function purchaseResource(
  warehouse: Warehouse,
  resource: ResourceDefinition,
  quantity: number,
  balance: Money,
): InventoryResult {
  if (!Number.isFinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РЈРєР°Р¶РёС‚Рµ РєРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РЅСѓР»СЏ.',
      warehouse,
      balance,
    };
  }

  const usedCapacity = getWarehouseUsedCapacity(warehouse);
  const freeCapacity = warehouse.capacity - usedCapacity;

  if (quantity > freeCapacity) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РќР° СЃРєР»Р°РґРµ РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РјРµСЃС‚Р°.',
      warehouse,
      balance,
    };
  }

  const spent = moneyMul(resource.unitPrice, quantity);

  if (moneyCmp(balance, spent) < 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІ.',
      warehouse,
      balance,
    };
  }

  const oldItem = warehouse.items[resource.id];
  const oldQuantity = oldItem?.quantity ?? 0;
  const oldValue = oldItem
    ? moneyMul(oldItem.averageUnitCost, oldQuantity)
    : ZERO_MONEY;

  const newQuantity = oldQuantity + quantity;
  const newTotalValue = moneyAdd(oldValue, spent);

  const averageUnitCost =
    newQuantity > 0
      ? moneyDiv(newTotalValue, newQuantity)
      : resource.unitPrice;

  const item: InventoryItem = {
    resourceId: resource.id,
    quantity: newQuantity,
    averageUnitCost,
  };

  return {
    ok: true,
    warehouse: {
      ...warehouse,
      items: {
        ...warehouse.items,
        [resource.id]: item,
      },
    },
    balance: moneySub(balance, spent),
    spent,
  };
}

export function removeResource(
  warehouse: Warehouse,
  resourceId: string,
  quantity: number,
): {
  ok: boolean;
  warehouse: Warehouse;
  removed: number;
  reason?: string;
} {
  const item = warehouse.items[resourceId];

  if (!item || quantity <= 0 || item.quantity < quantity) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      warehouse,
      removed: 0,
      reason: 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ С‚РѕРІР°СЂР° РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°РґРµ.',
    };
  }

  const remaining = item.quantity - quantity;
  const items = { ...warehouse.items };

  if (remaining <= 0.000001) {
    delete items[resourceId];
  } else {
    items[resourceId] = {
      ...item,
      quantity: remaining,
    };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    warehouse: {
      ...warehouse,
      items,
    },
    removed: quantity,
  };
}

export function consumeMonthlyResources(
  warehouse: Warehouse,
  requirements: ResourceRequirement[],
  businessLevel: number,
): ResourceConsumptionResult {
  let nextWarehouse = warehouse;
  let consumedValue: Money = ZERO_MONEY;
  const missingResources: ResourceConsumptionResult['missingResources'] = [];

  for (const requirement of requirements) {
    const required = requirement.unitsPerLevel * businessLevel;
    const item = nextWarehouse.items[requirement.resourceId];
    const available = item?.quantity ?? 0;
    const consumed = Math.min(required, available);

    if (consumed < required) {
      missingResources.push({
        resourceId: requirement.resourceId,
        required,
        available,
      });
    }

    if (consumed <= 0 || !item) {
      continue;
    }

    consumedValue = moneyAdd(
      consumedValue,
      moneyMul(item.averageUnitCost, consumed),
    );

    const result = removeResource(
      nextWarehouse,
      requirement.resourceId,
      consumed,
    );

    if (result.ok) {
      nextWarehouse = result.warehouse;
    }
  }

  return {
    warehouse: nextWarehouse,
    supplied: missingResources.length === 0,
    missingResources,
    consumedValue,
  };
}
export interface WarehouseWithdrawResult {
  ok: boolean;
  withdrawn: number;
  unitCost: number;
  totalCost: number;
  reason?: string;
}

/**
 * Р’Р·СЏС‚СЊ СЂРµСЃСѓСЂСЃ СЃРѕ СЃРєР»Р°РґР° РїРѕ СѓС‡С‘С‚РЅРѕР№ (СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРІР·РІРµС€РµРЅРЅРѕР№) СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё.
 * Р”РµРЅСЊРіРё РЅРµ СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ вЂ” РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РёР№ РєРѕРґ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃР°Рј СЂРµС€РёС‚СЊ, РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р»Рё СЌС‚Рѕ.
 * РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРµРіРѕ РїРµСЂРµРјРµС‰РµРЅРёСЏ В«СЃРєР»Р°Рґ РёРіСЂРѕРєР° в†’ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ/РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёРµВ».
 */
export interface WarehouseWithdrawResult {
  ok: boolean;
  warehouse: Warehouse;
  withdrawn: number;
  unitCost: number;
  totalCost: number;
  reason?: string;
}

/**
 * Р’Р·СЏС‚СЊ СЂРµСЃСѓСЂСЃ СЃРѕ СЃРєР»Р°РґР° РїРѕ СѓС‡С‘С‚РЅРѕР№ (СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРІР·РІРµС€РµРЅРЅРѕР№) СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё.
 * Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»С‘РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРєР»Р°Рґ. Р”РµРЅСЊРіРё РЅРµ РґРІРёРіР°РµС‚.
 */
export function withdrawFromWarehouse(
  warehouse: Warehouse,
  resourceId: string,
  requestedQty: number,
): WarehouseWithdrawResult {
  const item = warehouse.items[resourceId];
  if (!item || item.quantity <= 0 || requestedQty <= 0) {
    return { ok: false, warehouse, withdrawn: 0, unitCost: 0, totalCost: 0, reason: 'РќРµС‚ РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°РґРµ' };
  }
  const qty = Math.min(requestedQty, item.quantity);
  const unitCost = Number(item.averageUnitCost ?? 0);
  const removal = removeResource(warehouse, resourceId, qty);
  if (!removal.ok) {
    return { ok: false, warehouse, withdrawn: 0, unitCost: 0, totalCost: 0, reason: removal.reason };
  }
  return {
    ok: true,
    warehouse: removal.warehouse,
    withdrawn: qty,
    unitCost,
    totalCost: qty * unitCost,
  };
}



########## FINANCE: creditEngine.ts ##########
import type { LoanRuntime, Money } from '../domain/types';
import { moneyAdd, moneyCmp, moneyMax, moneyMin, moneyMul, moneySub, ZERO_MONEY } from '../domain/money';

export function monthlyRateForCreditScore(creditScore: number): number {
  if (creditScore < 300) return 0.03;
  if (creditScore <= 700) return 0.02;
  return 0.01;
}

export function estimateMonthlyPayment(principal: Money, monthlyRate: number, termMonths: number): Money {
  if (termMonths <= 0) return ZERO_MONEY;
  if (monthlyRate <= 0) return moneyMul(principal, 1 / termMonths);
  const denominator = 1 - Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, -termMonths);
  if (denominator <= 0) return ZERO_MONEY;
  return moneyMul(principal, monthlyRate / denominator);
}

export function calculateCreditLimit(cleanMonthlyProfit: Money, ratingCoefficient: number): Money {
  return moneyMax(ZERO_MONEY, moneyMul(cleanMonthlyProfit, ratingCoefficient));
}

export interface CreateLoanInput {
  amount: Money;
  monthlyProfit: Money;
  creditScore: number;
  ratingCoefficient: number;
  termMonths: number;
  currentMonth: number;
  existingLoan?: LoanRuntime | null;
}

export function createLoan(input: CreateLoanInput): { ok: true; loan: LoanRuntime } | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  if (input.existingLoan?.status === 'active') return { ok: false, reason: 'Нельзя иметь несколько активных кредитов.' };
  if (![3, 6, 12].includes(input.termMonths)) return { ok: false, reason: 'Доступны сроки 3, 6 или 12 месяцев.' };
  const limit = calculateCreditLimit(input.monthlyProfit, input.ratingCoefficient);
  if (moneyCmp(input.amount, ZERO_MONEY) <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'Сумма кредита должна быть больше нуля.' };
  if (moneyCmp(input.amount, limit) > 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'Запрошенная сумма выше кредитного лимита.' };
  const monthlyRate = monthlyRateForCreditScore(input.creditScore);
  return { ok: true, loan: {
    id: `loan-${input.currentMonth}-${Date.now()}`,
    originalPrincipal: input.amount, balance: input.amount, arrears: ZERO_MONEY,
    monthlyRate, monthlyPayment: estimateMonthlyPayment(input.amount, monthlyRate, input.termMonths),
    termMonths: input.termMonths, monthsRemaining: input.termMonths,
    overdueMonths: 0, issuedAtMonth: input.currentMonth, status: 'active',
  }};
}

export type LoanBankAction = 'none' | 'block_new_loans' | 'seize_business' | 'bankruptcy' | 'loan_paid';

export interface LoanMonthResult {
  loan: LoanRuntime; availableIncomeAfterPayment: Money;
  payment: Money; interest: Money; penalty: Money;
  creditScore: number; action: LoanBankAction;
}

function clampCreditScore(score: number): number { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1000, Math.round(score))); }

export function settleLoanMonth(loan: LoanRuntime, availableIncome: Money, creditScore: number): LoanMonthResult {
  if (loan.status !== 'active') return { loan, availableIncomeAfterPayment: availableIncome, payment: ZERO_MONEY, interest: ZERO_MONEY, penalty: ZERO_MONEY, creditScore, action: 'none' };
  const interest = moneyMul(loan.balance, loan.monthlyRate);
  const balanceWithInterest = moneyAdd(loan.balance, interest);
  const available = moneyMax(availableIncome, ZERO_MONEY);
  const payment = moneyMin(available, loan.monthlyPayment);
  const unpaid = moneySub(loan.monthlyPayment, payment);
  let nextBalance = moneyMax(ZERO_MONEY, moneySub(balanceWithInterest, payment));
  let nextArrears = loan.arrears; let nextOverdueMonths = loan.overdueMonths;
  let nextScore = creditScore; let penalty: Money = ZERO_MONEY; let action: LoanBankAction = 'none';
  if (moneyCmp(unpaid, ZERO_MONEY) > 0) {
    nextOverdueMonths += 1; nextArrears = moneyAdd(nextArrears, unpaid);
    if (nextOverdueMonths === 1) { penalty = moneyMul(nextBalance, 0.1); nextBalance = moneyAdd(nextBalance, penalty); nextScore -= 150; action = 'block_new_loans'; }
    else if (nextOverdueMonths === 2) { action = 'seize_business'; }
    else if (nextOverdueMonths >= 3) { action = 'bankruptcy'; }
  } else { nextOverdueMonths = 0; nextArrears = ZERO_MONEY; nextScore += 50; }
  const isPaid = moneyCmp(nextBalance, ZERO_MONEY) <= 0;
  let nextLoan: LoanRuntime = { ...loan, balance: nextBalance, arrears: nextArrears, overdueMonths: nextOverdueMonths, monthsRemaining: Math.max(0, loan.monthsRemaining - 1), status: isPaid ? 'paid' : 'active' };
  if (isPaid) { nextScore += 100; action = 'loan_paid'; }
  if (action === 'bankruptcy') { nextLoan = { ...nextLoan, status: 'defaulted' }; nextScore = 0; }
  return { loan: nextLoan, availableIncomeAfterPayment: moneySub(available, payment), payment, interest, penalty, creditScore: clampCreditScore(nextScore), action };
}

export function repayLoanEarly(loan: LoanRuntime, cash: Money, creditScore: number): { loan: LoanRuntime; cash: Money; creditScore: number; paid: Money; completed: boolean } {
  if (loan.status !== 'active') return { loan, cash, creditScore, paid: ZERO_MONEY, completed: false };
  const totalDebt = moneyAdd(loan.balance, loan.arrears);
  const paid = moneyMin(cash, totalDebt);
  const completed = moneyCmp(moneySub(totalDebt, paid), ZERO_MONEY) <= 0;
  return {
    loan: { ...loan, balance: completed ? ZERO_MONEY : moneySub(totalDebt, paid), arrears: ZERO_MONEY, status: completed ? 'paid' : 'active', overdueMonths: completed ? 0 : loan.overdueMonths },
    cash: moneySub(cash, paid), creditScore: clampCreditScore(creditScore + 75 + (completed ? 100 : 0)), paid, completed,
  };
}

export function monthlyCreditScoreGrowth(input: { score: number; companyLevel: number; companyAgeMonths: number; debtFree: boolean; positiveAssets: boolean }): number {
  let increase = 0;
  if (input.debtFree && input.positiveAssets) increase += 50;
  if (input.companyLevel >= 61) increase += 100; else if (input.companyLevel >= 31) increase += 60; else if (input.companyLevel >= 11) increase += 30; else if (input.companyLevel >= 1) increase += 10;
  if (input.companyAgeMonths > 0 && input.companyAgeMonths % 12 === 0) increase += 20;
  return clampCreditScore(input.score + increase);
}

export function addMoneyToBalance(balance: Money, amount: Money): Money { return moneyAdd(balance, amount); }




########## FINANCE: investmentEngine.ts ##########
import type { BondPosition, FundDefinition, FundPosition, Money } from '../domain/types';
import { moneyAdd, moneyMax, moneyMul, moneySub, ZERO_MONEY } from '../domain/money';

export function calculateMonthlyFundYield(annualYield: number): number {
  if (annualYield <= -1) return -1;
  return Math.pow(1 + annualYield, 1 / 12) - 1;
}

export function accrueFundMonth(position: FundPosition, definition: FundDefinition, random: () => number = Math.random): { position: FundPosition; income: Money; changeRate: number } {
  const expectedYield = calculateMonthlyFundYield(definition.annualYield);
  const noise = (random() * 2 - 1) * definition.monthlyVolatility;
  const changeRate = Math.max(-0.5, Math.min(0.5, expectedYield + noise));
  const nextValue = moneyMax(ZERO_MONEY, moneyMul(position.currentValue, 1 + changeRate));
  return { position: { ...position, currentValue: nextValue }, income: moneySub(nextValue, position.currentValue), changeRate };
}

export function addMoneyToFund(position: FundPosition | undefined, fundId: string, amount: Money): FundPosition {
  if (!position) return { fundId, invested: amount, currentValue: amount };
  return { ...position, invested: moneyAdd(position.invested, amount), currentValue: moneyAdd(position.currentValue, amount) };
}

export function withdrawFromFund(position: FundPosition, amount: Money): { ok: boolean; position: FundPosition; withdrawn: Money } {
  if (Number(position.currentValue) === 0 || Number(amount) <= 0) return { ok: false, position, withdrawn: ZERO_MONEY };
  const actual = moneyMax(ZERO_MONEY, moneySub(position.currentValue, amount));
  return { ok: true, position: { ...position, currentValue: actual }, withdrawn: moneySub(position.currentValue, actual) };
}

export function accrueBondMonth(position: BondPosition, annualCouponRate: number, faceValue: Money): { position: BondPosition; coupon: Money; matured: boolean } {
  const coupon = moneyMul(faceValue, (annualCouponRate / 12) * position.quantity);
  const monthsToMaturity = Math.max(0, position.monthsToMaturity - 1);
  return { position: { ...position, monthsToMaturity }, coupon, matured: monthsToMaturity === 0 };
}




########## FINANCE: adapters.ts ##########
// src/game/finance/adapters.ts
// РљРѕРЅРІРµСЂС‚Р°С†РёСЏ data/finance.ts в†’ domain/types.ts

import type {
  FundDefinition,
  BondDefinition,
  MetalPosition,
} from '../domain/types';
import {
  FUNDS,
  BONDS,
  METALS,
  METALS_BY_ID,
  type MetalId,
} from '../../data/finance';

/** РњРµСЃСЏС‡РЅР°СЏ РґРѕС…РѕРґРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ в†’ РіРѕРґРѕРІР°СЏ (СЃР»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕС†РµРЅС‚). */
function monthlyToAnnual(monthlyYield: number): number {
  return Math.pow(1 + monthlyYield, 12) - 1;
}

/** РљСѓРїРѕРЅ РІ РјРµСЃСЏС† в†’ РіРѕРґРѕРІРѕР№ (РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕР№). */
function monthlyCouponToAnnual(monthly: number): number {
  return monthly * 12;
}

export function buildFundDefinitions(): FundDefinition[] {
  return FUNDS.map((f) => ({
    id: f.id,
    name: f.name,
    annualYield: monthlyToAnnual(f.monthlyYield),
    monthlyVolatility: f.volatility,
  }));
}

export const FUND_DEFINITIONS_BY_ID: Record<string, FundDefinition> =
  Object.fromEntries(buildFundDefinitions().map((f) => [f.id, f]));

export function buildBondDefinitions(): BondDefinition[] {
  return BONDS.map((b) => ({
    id: b.id,
    name: b.name,
    annualCouponRate: monthlyCouponToAnnual(b.monthlyCoupon),
    maturityMonths: b.termMonths,
    faceValue: String(b.faceValue),
    purchasePrice: String(b.faceValue),
  }));
}

export const BOND_DEFINITIONS_BY_ID: Record<string, BondDefinition> =
  Object.fromEntries(buildBondDefinitions().map((b) => [b.id, b]));

/** РќР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РјРµС‚Р°Р»Р»Р° (С†РµРЅР° = basePrice). */
export function createMetalPosition(
  metalId: string,
  quantity: number,
): MetalPosition {
  const def = METALS_BY_ID[metalId as MetalId];
  return {
    metalId,
    quantity,
    currentPrice: String(def?.basePrice ?? 0),
  };
}

/** РР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ С†РµРЅС‹ РјРµС‚Р°Р»Р»Р° Р·Р° РјРµСЃСЏС† РїРѕ РІРѕР»Р°С‚РёР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё. */
export function tickMetalPrice(
  metalId: string,
  currentPrice: string,
  random: () => number = Math.random,
): string {
  const def = METALS_BY_ID[metalId as MetalId];
  if (!def) return currentPrice;
  const noise = (random() * 2 - 1) * def.volatility;
  const next = Math.max(1, Number(currentPrice) * (1 + noise));
  return String(Math.round(next * 100) / 100);
}

/** РћРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ РјРµС‚Р°Р»Р»Р° РїРѕ id. */
export function getMetalDef(metalId: string) {
  return METALS_BY_ID[metalId as MetalId];
}

export function getAllMetals() {
  return METALS;
}



########## MARKET: marketEngine.ts ##########
import type {
  MarketCompany,
  PricePoint,
} from '../domain/types';
import {
  moneyCmp,
  moneyDiv,
  moneyMax,
  moneyMul,
} from '../domain/money';

const MIN_SHARE_PRICE = '0.01';
const GAME_MINUTES_PER_MONTH = 10;
const GAME_MONTHS_PER_YEAR = 12;

const VOLATILITY_BY_PROFILE: Record<
  MarketCompany['volatility'],
  number
> = {
  stable: 0.001,
  moderate: 0.004,
  volatile: 0.012,
  speculative: 0.035,
};

export interface MarketTickOptions {
  minuteIndex: number;
  monthIndex: number;
  random?: () => number;
  companySignals?: Record<string, number>;
}

export function tickMarket(
  companies: MarketCompany[],
  options: MarketTickOptions,
): MarketCompany[] {
  const random = options.random ?? Math.random;
  const companySignals = options.companySignals ?? {};

  return companies.map((company) => {
    const randomNoise =
      (random() * 2 - 1) *
      VOLATILITY_BY_PROFILE[company.volatility];

    const annualGrowth = Math.max(
      -0.95,
      Math.min(5, company.annualGrowth),
    );

    const ticksPerYear =
      GAME_MONTHS_PER_YEAR * GAME_MINUTES_PER_MONTH;

    const baseDrift =
      Math.pow(1 + annualGrowth, 1 / ticksPerYear) - 1;

    const companySignal =
      companySignals[company.id] ?? 0;

    const returnThisTick =
      baseDrift + randomNoise + companySignal;

    const multiplier = Math.max(
      0.05,
      Math.min(1.5, 1 + returnThisTick),
    );

    const nextPrice = moneyMax(
      MIN_SHARE_PRICE,
      moneyMul(company.price, multiplier),
    );

    const relativeChange = Number(
      moneyDiv(nextPrice, company.price),
    );

    const lastChangePct = Number.isFinite(relativeChange)
      ? (relativeChange - 1) * 100
      : 0;

    const nextPoint: PricePoint = {
      minuteIndex: options.minuteIndex,
      monthIndex: options.monthIndex,
      price: nextPrice,
    };

    return {
      ...company,
      price: nextPrice,
      lastChangePct,
      history: [...company.history, nextPoint].slice(-100),
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Р РµРґРєРёРµ РєСЂСѓРїРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕР»РµР±Р°РЅРёСЏ СЃРїРµРєСѓР»СЏС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… Р°РєС†РёР№.
 * Р’С‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р· РІ РєРѕРЅС†Рµ РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°.
 */
export function applySpeculativeMonthShock(
  company: MarketCompany,
  random: () => number = Math.random,
): MarketCompany {
  if (company.volatility !== 'speculative') {
    return company;
  }

  const roll = random();

  let multiplier = 1;

  if (roll < 0.01) {
    multiplier = 10;
  } else if (roll > 0.99) {
    multiplier = 0.2;
  }

  if (multiplier === 1) {
    return company;
  }

  const oldPrice = company.price;
  const nextPrice = moneyMax(
    MIN_SHARE_PRICE,
    moneyMul(oldPrice, multiplier),
  );

  return {
    ...company,
    price: nextPrice,
    lastChangePct:
      multiplier === 10 ? 900 : -80,
  };
}

export function calculateMonthlyDividendPerShare(
  company: MarketCompany,
): string {
  const monthlyYield =
    company.annualDividendYield /
    GAME_MONTHS_PER_YEAR;

  return moneyMul(company.price, monthlyYield);
}

export function getCompanyPriceChange(
  company: MarketCompany,
): number {
  if (company.history.length < 2) {
    return 0;
  }

  const previous =
    company.history[company.history.length - 2];

  if (moneyCmp(previous.price, '0') <= 0) {
    return 0;
  }

  const ratio = Number(
    moneyDiv(company.price, previous.price),
  );

  return Number.isFinite(ratio)
    ? (ratio - 1) * 100
    : 0;
}



########## MARKET: build.ts ##########
// src/game/market/build.ts
// РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ MarketCompany[] РёР· COMPANIES (data/companies.ts).
// Р•РґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅРѕРµ РјРµСЃС‚Рѕ, РіРґРµ Р¶РёРІС‘С‚ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ СЂС‹РЅРєР°.

import type { MarketCompany } from '../domain/types';
import { COMPANIES } from '../../data/companies';

/** Р“РѕРґРѕРІРѕР№ СЂРѕСЃС‚ РїРѕ С‚РёРїСѓ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ. */
const ANNUAL_GROWTH_BY_BEHAVIOR: Record<string, number> = {
  stable: 0.08,
  moderate: 0.15,
  volatile: 0.25,
  speculative: 0.40,
};

/** Р“РѕРґРѕРІР°СЏ РґРёРІРёРґРµРЅРґРЅР°СЏ РґРѕС…РѕРґРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё dividends: true). */
const ANNUAL_DIVIDEND_BY_BEHAVIOR: Record<string, number> = {
  stable: 0.04,
  moderate: 0.03,
  volatile: 0.02,
  speculative: 0.0,
};

export function buildMarketCompanies(): MarketCompany[] {
  return COMPANIES.map((c) => ({
    id: c.id,
    name: c.name,
    sectorId: c.sector,
    price: String(c.basePrice),
    history: [
      { minuteIndex: 0, monthIndex: 0, price: String(c.basePrice) },
    ],
    lastChangePct: 0,
    volatility: c.stockBehavior,
    annualGrowth: ANNUAL_GROWTH_BY_BEHAVIOR[c.stockBehavior] ?? 0.1,
    annualDividendYield: c.dividends
      ? ANNUAL_DIVIDEND_BY_BEHAVIOR[c.stockBehavior] ?? 0
      : 0,
    monthlyRevenue: '0',
    monthlyProfit: '0',
    debt: '0',
    reputation: 50 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30),
    marketShare:
      c.size === 'large'
        ? 0.15 + Math.random() * 0.15
        : 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.08,
    competitorEligible: c.roles.includes('competitor'),
    isActiveCompetitor: false,
  }));
}



########## MARKET: trading.ts ##########
import type {
  Money,
  StockPosition,
} from '../domain/types';
import {
  moneyAdd,
  moneyCmp,
  moneyMax,
  moneyMul,
  moneySub,
  ZERO_MONEY,
} from '../domain/money';

export interface TradePolicy {
  commissionRate: number;
  capitalGainsTaxRate: number;
}

export interface TradeResult {
  cash: Money;
  position: StockPosition | null;

  grossAmount: Money;
  commission: Money;
  tax: Money;
  realizedPnl: Money;
}

function normalizeShares(shares: number): number {
  return Math.round(shares * 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;
}

export function buyShares(input: {
  cash: Money;
  companyId: string;
  currentPrice: Money;
  shares: number;
  existingPosition?: StockPosition;
  policy: TradePolicy;
}):
  | { ok: true; result: TradeResult }
  | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  const shares = normalizeShares(input.shares);

  if (!Number.isFinite(shares) || shares <= 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ Р°РєС†РёР№ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РЅСѓР»СЏ.',
    };
  }

  if (moneyCmp(input.currentPrice, ZERO_MONEY) <= 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅР°СЏ С†РµРЅР° Р°РєС†РёРё.',
    };
  }

  const grossAmount = moneyMul(input.currentPrice, shares);
  const commission = moneyMul(
    grossAmount,
    input.policy.commissionRate,
  );
  const totalCost = moneyAdd(grossAmount, commission);

  if (moneyCmp(input.cash, totalCost) < 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РќРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ РґРµРЅРµРі РґР»СЏ РїРѕРєСѓРїРєРё.',
    };
  }

  const oldPosition = input.existingPosition;
  const oldShares = oldPosition?.shares ?? 0;
  const nextShares = oldShares + shares;

  const oldCost = oldPosition
    ? moneyMul(oldPosition.averageBuyPrice, oldShares)
    : ZERO_MONEY;

  const totalBasis = moneyAdd(
    oldCost,
    moneyAdd(grossAmount, commission),
  );

  const averageBuyPrice =
    nextShares > 0
      ? moneyMul(totalBasis, 1 / nextShares)
      : input.currentPrice;

  const position: StockPosition = {
    companyId: input.companyId,
    shares: nextShares,
    averageBuyPrice,
    realizedPnl: oldPosition?.realizedPnl ?? ZERO_MONEY,
  };

  return {
    ok: true,
    result: {
      cash: moneySub(input.cash, totalCost),
      position,
      grossAmount,
      commission,
      tax: ZERO_MONEY,
      realizedPnl: ZERO_MONEY,
    },
  };
}

export function sellShares(input: {
  cash: Money;
  companyId: string;
  currentPrice: Money;
  shares: number;
  position: StockPosition;
  policy: TradePolicy;
}):
  | { ok: true; result: TradeResult }
  | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  const shares = normalizeShares(input.shares);

  if (!Number.isFinite(shares) || shares <= 0) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'РљРѕР»РёС‡РµСЃС‚РІРѕ Р°РєС†РёР№ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РЅСѓР»СЏ.',
    };
  }

  if (shares > input.position.shares) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      reason: 'Р’ РїРѕСЂС‚С„РµР»Рµ РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕ С‚Р°РєРёС… Р°РєС†РёР№.',
    };
  }

  const grossAmount = moneyMul(input.currentPrice, shares);
  const costBasis = moneyMul(
    input.position.averageBuyPrice,
    shares,
  );

  const commission = moneyMul(
    grossAmount,
    input.policy.commissionRate,
  );

  const taxableProfit = moneyMax(
    moneySub(grossAmount, costBasis),
    ZERO_MONEY,
  );

  const tax = moneyMul(
    taxableProfit,
    input.policy.capitalGainsTaxRate,
  );

  const netProceeds = moneySub(
    moneySub(grossAmount, commission),
    tax,
  );

  const realizedPnl = moneySub(
    moneySub(grossAmount, costBasis),
    moneyAdd(commission, tax),
  );

  const remainingShares = normalizeShares(
    input.position.shares - shares,
  );

  const nextPosition =
    remainingShares <= 0
      ? null
      : {
          ...input.position,
          shares: remainingShares,
          realizedPnl: moneyAdd(
            input.position.realizedPnl,
            realizedPnl,
          ),
        };

  return {
    ok: true,
    result: {
      cash: moneyAdd(input.cash, netProceeds),
      position: nextPosition,
      grossAmount,
      commission,
      tax,
      realizedPnl,
    },
  };
}



########## CONTRACTS: contractEngine.ts ##########
import type { ContractRuntime, Money, Warehouse } from '../domain/types';
import { moneyAdd, moneyCmp, ZERO_MONEY } from '../domain/money';
import { removeResource } from '../logistics/inventory';

export function acceptContract(contract: ContractRuntime, currentMonth: number): ContractRuntime {
  if (contract.status !== 'available') return contract;
  if (currentMonth > contract.deadlineMonth) return { ...contract, status: 'failed' };
  return { ...contract, status: 'active' };
}

export function deliverContractGoods(input: {
  contract: ContractRuntime; warehouse: Warehouse; requestedQuantity: number; currentMonth: number; balance: Money;
}): { ok: true; contract: ContractRuntime; warehouse: Warehouse; balance: Money; deliveredNow: number; completed: boolean } | { ok: false; reason: string; contract: ContractRuntime; warehouse: Warehouse; balance: Money } {
  const { contract } = input;
  if (contract.status !== 'active') return { ok: false, reason: 'Контракт не активен.', contract, warehouse: input.warehouse, balance: input.balance };
  if (input.currentMonth > contract.deadlineMonth) return { ok: false, reason: 'Срок контракта истёк.', contract: { ...contract, status: 'failed' }, warehouse: input.warehouse, balance: input.balance };
  const remaining = contract.requiredQuantity - contract.deliveredQuantity;
  const quantity = Math.min(remaining, input.requestedQuantity);
  if (quantity <= 0) return { ok: false, reason: 'Нечего доставлять.', contract, warehouse: input.warehouse, balance: input.balance };
  const removal = removeResource(input.warehouse, contract.resourceId, quantity);
  if (!removal.ok) return { ok: false, reason: removal.reason ?? 'Не удалось списать товар.', contract, warehouse: input.warehouse, balance: input.balance };
  const deliveredQuantity = contract.deliveredQuantity + quantity;
  const completed = deliveredQuantity >= contract.requiredQuantity;
  const nextContract: ContractRuntime = { ...contract, deliveredQuantity, status: completed ? 'completed' : 'active' };
  const nextBalance = completed ? moneyAdd(input.balance, contract.reward) : input.balance;
  return { ok: true, contract: nextContract, warehouse: removal.warehouse, balance: nextBalance, deliveredNow: quantity, completed };
}

export function expireContracts(contracts: ContractRuntime[], currentMonth: number): ContractRuntime[] {
  return contracts.map((c) => (c.status === 'active' && currentMonth > c.deadlineMonth) ? { ...c, status: 'failed' } : c);
}

export function getContractProgress(contract: ContractRuntime): number {
  if (contract.requiredQuantity <= 0) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, contract.deliveredQuantity / contract.requiredQuantity));
}

export function hasContractDeadlinePassed(contract: ContractRuntime, currentMonth: number): boolean {
  return contract.status === 'active' && currentMonth > contract.deadlineMonth && moneyCmp(contract.reward, ZERO_MONEY) >= 0;
}




########## CONTRACTS: adapters.ts ##########
// src/game/contracts/adapters.ts
// Р“РµРЅРµСЂР°С‚РѕСЂ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚РѕРІ РёР· data/contracts.ts (CONTRACT_TEMPLATES).

import type { ContractRuntime } from '../domain/types';
import { computeContractReward, type ContractSize } from '../../data/contracts';
import { RESOURCES } from '../../data/resources';
import { STOCKS } from '../../data/stocks';

const AVAILABLE_RESOURCES = RESOURCES.filter((r) => r.group !== 'equipment');

export function generateContracts(
  currentMonth: number,
  count = 2,
): ContractRuntime[] {
  const out: ContractRuntime[] = [];
  if (AVAILABLE_RESOURCES.length === 0) return out;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const resource =
      AVAILABLE_RESOURCES[Math.floor(Math.random() * AVAILABLE_RESOURCES.length)];
    const size: ContractSize = Math.random() < 0.7 ? 'small' : 'large';
    const durationRange = size === 'small' ? [3, 3] : [6, 12];
    const duration =
      durationRange[0] +
      Math.floor(Math.random() * (durationRange[1] - durationRange[0] + 1));

    const quantity =
      size === 'small'
        ? 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40)
        : 50 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 200);

    // Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІР°СЏ РјРµСЃСЏС‡РЅР°СЏ РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєР° РґР»СЏ СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚Р° РЅР°РіСЂР°РґС‹ вЂ” 100k
    const { reward } = computeContractReward(size, 100_000, duration);

    const client =
      STOCKS.length > 0
        ? STOCKS[Math.floor(Math.random() * STOCKS.length)]
        : null;

    out.push({
      id: `contract_${currentMonth}_${Date.now()}_${i}_${Math.random()
        .toString(36)
        .slice(2, 6)}`,
      title: client ? `${client.name}: ${resource.name}` : `РљРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚: ${resource.name}`,
      description: `Р”РѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ ${quantity} ${resource.unit} В«${resource.name}В» Р·Р° ${duration} РјРµСЃ.`,
      clientCompanyId: client?.id,
      resourceId: resource.id,
      requiredQuantity: quantity,
      deliveredQuantity: 0,
      reward: String(Math.round(reward)),
      createdAtMonth: currentMonth,
      deadlineMonth: currentMonth + duration,
      status: 'available',
    });
  }

  return out;
}



########## TIME: clock.ts ##########
import type { ClockState } from '../domain/types';

export const GAME_MONTH_DURATION_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

export const DEFAULT_SPEEDS = [0, 1, 3, 6] as const;

export interface ClockAdvanceResult {
  clock: ClockState;
  completedMonths: number[];
}

export function createInitialClock(): ClockState {
  return {
    monthIndex: 0,
    monthProgressMs: 0,
    speed: 1,
    paused: false,
  };
}

export function advanceClock(
  clock: ClockState,
  realDeltaMs: number,
): ClockAdvanceResult {
  if (clock.paused || clock.speed <= 0 || realDeltaMs <= 0) {
    return {
      clock,
      completedMonths: [],
    };
  }

  const safeDelta = Number.isFinite(realDeltaMs)
    ? Math.max(0, realDeltaMs)
    : 0;

  const gameDelta = safeDelta * clock.speed;
  const totalProgress = clock.monthProgressMs + gameDelta;

  const monthsCompleted = Math.floor(
    totalProgress / GAME_MONTH_DURATION_MS,
  );

  const monthProgressMs =
    totalProgress % GAME_MONTH_DURATION_MS;

  const completedMonths = Array.from(
    { length: monthsCompleted },
    (_, index) => clock.monthIndex + index + 1,
  );

  return {
    clock: {
      ...clock,
      monthIndex: clock.monthIndex + monthsCompleted,
      monthProgressMs,
    },
    completedMonths,
  };
}

export function setClockPaused(
  clock: ClockState,
  paused: boolean,
): ClockState {
  return {
    ...clock,
    paused,
  };
}

export function setClockSpeed(
  clock: ClockState,
  speed: number,
): ClockState {
  // GDD: С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ 0 (РїР°СѓР·Р°), 1, 3, 6. РћСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј,
  // С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РєРЅРѕРїРєРё РІ UI РІСЃРµРіРґР° СЃРѕРѕС‚РІРµС‚СЃС‚РІРѕРІР°Р»Рё СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕР№ СЃРєРѕСЂРѕСЃС‚Рё.
  const allowed = [0, 1, 3, 6];
  return {
    ...clock,
    speed: allowed.includes(speed) ? speed : clock.speed,
  };
}

export function getMonthLabel(monthIndex: number): string {
  const currentMonth = monthIndex % 12 + 1;
  const currentYear = Math.floor(monthIndex / 12) + 1;

  return `Р“РѕРґ ${currentYear}, РјРµСЃСЏС† ${currentMonth}`;
}

export function getMonthProgress(clock: ClockState): number {
  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      clock.monthProgressMs / GAME_MONTH_DURATION_MS,
    ),
  );
}



########## TIME: clockStore.ts ##########
// src/game/simulation/clockStore.ts
// РћС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‚РѕСЂ РґР»СЏ РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё. РќРµ СЃРјРµС€РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃ gameStore:
// РІСЂРµРјСЏ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РјРµС‚Р°-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ, Р° РЅРµ РёРіСЂРѕРІС‹Рµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ.

import { create } from 'zustand';
import type { ClockState } from '../domain/types';
import {
  advanceClock,
  createInitialClock,
  setClockPaused,
  setClockSpeed,
} from './clock';

interface ClockStore {
  clock: ClockState;

  setPaused: (paused: boolean) => void;
  togglePause: () => void;
  setSpeed: (speed: number) => void;
  /** РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРЅСѓС‚СЊ РІСЂРµРјСЏ РЅР° deltaMs, РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅРЅС‹С… РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ. */
  advance: (deltaMs: number) => number[];
  /** Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ clock С†РµР»РёРєРѕРј (РґР»СЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ). */
  load: (clock: ClockState) => void;
  reset: () => void;
}

export const useClockStore = create<ClockStore>((set, get) => ({
  clock: createInitialClock(),

  setPaused: (paused) =>
    set({ clock: setClockPaused(get().clock, paused) }),

  togglePause: () =>
    set({ clock: setClockPaused(get().clock, !get().clock.paused) }),

  setSpeed: (speed) =>
    set({ clock: setClockSpeed(get().clock, speed) }),

  advance: (deltaMs) => {
    const { clock, completedMonths } = advanceClock(get().clock, deltaMs);
    if (completedMonths.length > 0 || clock.monthProgressMs !== get().clock.monthProgressMs) {
      set({ clock });
    }
    return completedMonths;
  },

  load: (clock) => set({ clock }),

  reset: () => set({ clock: createInitialClock() }),
}));



########## TIME: monthlyKernel.ts ##########
export interface MonthlyProcessor<State> {
  id: string;
  order: number;
  run: (state: State, completedMonth: number) => State;
}

export function processCompletedMonths<State>(
  initialState: State,
  completedMonths: number[],
  processors: MonthlyProcessor<State>[],
): State {
  const orderedProcessors = [...processors].sort(
    (a, b) => a.order - b.order,
  );

  let state = initialState;

  for (const month of completedMonths) {
    for (const processor of orderedProcessors) {
      state = processor.run(state, month);
    }
  }

  return state;
}



########## TIME: useSimulationLoop.ts ##########
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

interface SimulationLoopOptions {
  paused: boolean;
  enabled?: boolean;
  onAdvance: (realDeltaMs: number) => void;
}

export function useSimulationLoop({
  paused,
  enabled = true,
  onAdvance,
}: SimulationLoopOptions): void {
  const callbackRef = useRef(onAdvance);

  useEffect(() => {
    callbackRef.current = onAdvance;
  }, [onAdvance]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled || typeof window === 'undefined') {
      return;
    }

    let animationFrame = 0;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    const resetTime = () => {
      lastTime = performance.now();
    };

    const frame = (now: number) => {
      const delta = Math.max(0, Math.min(1000, now - lastTime));
      lastTime = now;

      const pageVisible =
        typeof document === 'undefined' ||
        document.visibilityState === 'visible';

      if (!paused && pageVisible && delta > 0) {
        callbackRef.current(delta);
      }

      animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', resetTime);
    animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(frame);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrame);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', resetTime);
    };
  }, [enabled, paused]);
}



########## BUSINESS: adapters.ts ##########
// src/game/business/adapters.ts
// РђРґР°РїС‚РµСЂС‹ РјРµР¶РґСѓ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРјРё РґР°РЅРЅС‹РјРё (BusinessDef РёР· data/businesses.ts)
// Рё СЂР°РЅС‚Р°Р№Рј-СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµРј (BusinessRuntime РёР· domain/types.ts).
// Р—РґРµСЃСЊ Р¶Рµ вЂ” РјР°РїРїРёРЅРі ResourceDef в†’ ResourceDefinition РґР»СЏ inventory.

import type {
  BusinessRuntime,
  Id,
  ResourceDefinition,
  ResourceKind,
  ResourceRequirement,
  Warehouse,
} from '../domain/types';
import type { BusinessDef, BusinessKind } from '../../data/businesses';
import type { ResourceDef } from '../../data/resources';
import { RESOURCES_BY_ID } from '../../data/resources';
import { createWarehouse } from '../logistics/inventory';
import { TAXES } from '../config/taxes';


const SECTOR_BY_KIND: Record<BusinessKind, string> = {
  service: 'services',
  retail: 'retail',
  production: 'production',
  logistics: 'logistics',
  office: 'office',
};

const KIND_MAP: Record<string, ResourceKind> = {
  raw: 'raw',
  goods: 'finished',
  equipment: 'equipment',
  food: 'food',
  fuel: 'raw',
};

function mapResourceKind(group: string): ResourceKind {
  return KIND_MAP[group] ?? 'raw';
}

/** РџСЂРµРѕР±СЂР°Р·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ ResourceDef РёР· data РІ ResourceDefinition РёР· domain. */
export function resourceDefToDomain(res: ResourceDef): ResourceDefinition {
  return {
    id: res.id,
    name: res.name,
    category: res.group,
    unit: res.unit,
    kind: mapResourceKind(res.group),
    unitPrice: String(res.basePrice ?? 0),
  };
}

/** РР·РІР»РµС‡СЊ ResourceRequirement[] РёР· BusinessDef.keyResources. */
export function resourceRequirementsFromDef(def: BusinessDef): ResourceRequirement[] {
  return Object.entries(def.keyResources).map(([resourceId, unitsPerLevel]) => ({
    resourceId,
    unitsPerLevel,
  }));
}

/**
 * РЎРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ BusinessRuntime РёР· BusinessDef.
 * @param instanceId РЈРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ ID СЌРєР·РµРјРїР»СЏСЂР° (РїРѕР·РІРѕР»СЏРµС‚ РЅРµСЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РѕРґРёРЅР°РєРѕРІС‹С…).
 */
export function businessDefToRuntime(
  def: BusinessDef,
  opts: {
    instanceId?: Id;
    level?: number;
    premisesMode?: 'rent' | 'owned';
  } = {},
): BusinessRuntime {
  const level = opts.level ?? 1;
  const premisesMode = opts.premisesMode ?? 'rent';
  const instanceId =
    opts.instanceId ?? `biz_${def.id}_${Date.now().toString(36)}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;

  // РђСЂРµРЅРґР° = 70% fixedCosts, Р–РљРҐ = 30%, РѕР±СЃР»СѓР¶РёРІР°РЅРёРµ = 5% fixedCosts
  const rentMonthly = premisesMode === 'rent'
    ? String(def.fixedCosts * 0.7)
    : '0';
  const utilitiesMonthly = String(def.fixedCosts * 0.3);
  const maintenanceMonthly = String(def.fixedCosts * 0.05);

  return {
    id: instanceId,
    defId: def.id,
    name: def.name,
    sectorId: SECTOR_BY_KIND[def.kind] ?? def.kind,
    level,
    status: 'operating',
    cashBalance: '0',
    baseMonthlyRevenue: String(def.baseRevenue),
    demandMultiplier: 1,
    productivityMultiplier: 1,
    monthlyPayroll: '0', // РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ payrollProcessor РїРѕ С„Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРј СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєР°Рј
    monthlyRent: rentMonthly,
    monthlyUtilities: utilitiesMonthly,
    monthlyMaintenance: maintenanceMonthly,
    taxRate: TAXES.businessProfit,
    resourceNeeds: resourceRequirementsFromDef(def),
    monthsWithoutSupply: 0,
    monthsStopped: 0,
    accruedLiabilities: '0',
  };
}

/** РќР°С‡Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃРєР»Р°Рґ РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃР° вЂ” СЃС‚РѕРє РЅР° 1 РјРµСЃСЏС†. */
export function startingWarehouseFor(
  def: BusinessDef,
  capacity = 10_000,
): Warehouse {
  const w = createWarehouse(capacity);
  for (const [id, qty] of Object.entries(def.keyResources)) {
    const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[id];
    if (!res) continue;
    w.items[id] = {
      resourceId: id,
      quantity: qty,
      averageUnitCost: String(res.basePrice ?? 0),
    };
  }
  return w;
}

/** РќР°Р№С‚Рё BusinessDef РїРѕ BusinessRuntime (С‡РµСЂРµР· defId). */
export function findDefForRuntime(
  runtime: BusinessRuntime,
  allDefs: BusinessDef[],
): BusinessDef | undefined {
  if (runtime.defId) return allDefs.find((d) => d.id === runtime.defId);
  // Fallback: РїРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓРµРј СѓРіР°РґР°С‚СЊ РїРѕ id (РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹С… СЃРЅРёРјРєРѕРІ)
  return allDefs.find((d) => runtime.id.startsWith(`biz_${d.id}_`));
}
// в”Ђв”Ђ РћС†РµРЅРєР° СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ

export interface OpeningCostEstimate {
  equipment: number;
  premises: number;
  firstMonthRent: number;
  firstMonthUtilities: number;
  operatingCapital: number;
  total: number;
}

export function estimateOpeningCost(
  def: BusinessDef,
  premisesMode: 'rent' | 'owned',
  operatingCapital: number,
): OpeningCostEstimate {
  const equipment = def.startingEquipment ?? def.fixedCosts * 2;
  const premises =
    premisesMode === 'owned'
      ? def.premisesPrice ?? def.fixedCosts * 24
      : 0;
  const firstMonthRent = premisesMode === 'rent' ? def.fixedCosts * 0.7 : 0;
  const firstMonthUtilities = def.fixedCosts * 0.3;
  const total =
    equipment + premises + firstMonthRent + firstMonthUtilities + operatingCapital;

  return {
    equipment,
    premises,
    firstMonthRent,
    firstMonthUtilities,
    operatingCapital,
    total,
  };
}

  research_specialist: 'РСЃСЃР»РµРґРѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊ',

const buildWorkerDefs = (roles: EmployeeRole[], tier: 't1' | 't2' | 't3'): EmployeeDef[] =>
  roles.map((role) => ({
    role,
    name: WORKER_NAME_MAP[role] ?? role,
    description: 'Р Р°Р±РѕС‡РёР№ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р»',
    salaryMin: TIER_SALARY[tier][0],
    salaryMax: TIER_SALARY[tier][1],
    contractMinMonths: 3,
    contractMaxMonths: 12,
  }));

export const EMPLOYEE_ROLES: EmployeeDef[] = [
  ...MANAGEMENT_DEFS,
  ...buildWorkerDefs(WORKER_TIER_1, 't1'),
  ...buildWorkerDefs(WORKER_TIER_2, 't2'),
  ...buildWorkerDefs(WORKER_TIER_3, 't3'),
];

export const EMPLOYEE_ROLES_BY_ID: Record<EmployeeRole, EmployeeDef> = Object.fromEntries(
  EMPLOYEE_ROLES.map((e) => [e.role, e]),
) as Record<EmployeeRole, EmployeeDef>;

export const EMPLOYEE_RULES = {
  maxPerRolePerBusiness: 1,
  fireChanceByMonths: [
    { upTo: 6,  chance: 0.05 },
    { upTo: 12, chance: 0.10 },
    { upTo: 18, chance: 0.15 },
    { upTo: 24, chance: 0.20 },
    { upTo: Infinity, chance: 0.50 },
  ] as Array<{ upTo: number; chance: number }>,
  trainingBaseCost: 50_000,
  maxQualification: 5,
};

/** РҐРµР»РїРµСЂ: СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰Р°СЏ Р»Рё СЂРѕР»СЊ (РЅРµ Р±РѕР»РµРµ 1 РЅР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ). */
export function isManagementRole(role: EmployeeRole): boolean {
  return (MANAGEMENT_ROLES as readonly string[]).includes(role);
}



########## DATA: stocks.ts ##########
// src/data/stocks.ts
// РџР РћРР—Р’РћР”РќР«Р™ С„Р°Р№Р»: РµРґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє РїСЂР°РІРґС‹ вЂ” companies.ts.
// Р—РґРµСЃСЊ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ Р°РєС†РёР№ (С†РµРЅР°, РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ) Рё С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ СЂС‹РЅРѕС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ С‚РёРєР°.
// РўРёРє РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ РёР· СЃРёРјСѓР»СЏС†РёРѕРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ СЏРґСЂР° (Р‘Р»РѕРє 1), Р° РЅРµ РёР· App.tsx.

import { COMPANIES, type SectorId, type StockBehavior } from './companies';

export type { SectorId, StockBehavior };

export interface Stock {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  category: SectorId;
  sector: SectorId;
  behavior: StockBehavior;
  dividends: boolean;
  price: number;
  prevPrice: number;
  basePrice: number;
  /** РџРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёРµ 100 С‚РѕС‡РµРє С†РµРЅС‹ (РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЏ С‚РµРєСѓС‰СѓСЋ) */
  history: number[];
  /** РЈСЃС‚Р°СЂРµРІС€РµРµ РїРѕР»Рµ РґР»СЏ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃ UI, РµСЃР»Рё РіРґРµ-С‚Рѕ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ */
    volatility?: number;
  /** @deprecated РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ UI, СѓРґР°Р»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ Р‘Р»РѕРєРµ 9 */
  emoji?: string;
  /** @deprecated РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ UI, СѓРґР°Р»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РІ Р‘Р»РѕРєРµ 9 */
  ticker?: string;
}

/** Р’РѕР»Р°С‚РёР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ Р·Р° РѕРґРёРЅ СЂС‹РЅРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚РёРє (в‰€0.1 РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°). */
export const BEHAVIOR_VOLATILITY: Record<StockBehavior, number> = {
  stable: 0.003,
  moderate: 0.008,
  volatile: 0.02,
  speculative: 0.05,
};

/** РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚РѕРІРѕРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ СЂС‹РЅРєР° вЂ” СЃРѕР±РёСЂР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РёР· РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРіР° РєРѕРјРїР°РЅРёР№. */
export const STOCKS: Stock[] = COMPANIES.map(c => ({
  id: c.id,
  name: c.name,
  category: c.sector,
  sector: c.sector,
  behavior: c.stockBehavior,
  dividends: c.dividends,
  price: c.basePrice,
  prevPrice: c.basePrice,
  basePrice: c.basePrice,
  history: [c.basePrice],
  volatility: BEHAVIOR_VOLATILITY[c.stockBehavior],
  emoji: 'рџ“€',
  ticker: c.id.slice(0, 4).toUpperCase(),
}));

/**
 * РћРґРёРЅ СЂС‹РЅРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ С‚РёРє: РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ С†РµРЅС‹ РїРѕ С‚РёРїСѓ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ.

export function categoryLabel(category: string): string {
  return CATEGORY_LABELS[category] ?? category;
}

export function categoryIcon(category: string): string {
  return CATEGORY_ICONS[category] ?? 'вЂў';
}



########## STORE: ключевые функции ##########


----- store.ts: recordTransaction -----
  recordTransaction: (input: {
    direction: LedgerDirection;
    amount: Money | number;
    category: string;
    description: string;
    allowNegative?: boolean;
    relatedEntityId?: string;
  }) => { ok: boolean; reason?: string };

  /** РџРѕР»СѓС‡РёС‚СЊ РґРѕС…РѕРґ/СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ Р·Р° РјРµСЃСЏС† РёР· Р¶СѓСЂРЅР°Р»Р°. */
  getMonthLedger: (monthIndex: number) => {
    income: Money;
    expenses: Money;
    net: Money;
    entries: LedgerEntry[];
  };
    /** Р”РѕС…РѕРґ/СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ Р·Р° РјРµСЃСЏС†, СЃРіСЂСѓРїРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕ РєР°С‚РµРіРѕСЂРёСЏРј. */
  getMonthLedgerByCategory: (monthIndex: number) => {
    income: Record<string, Money>;
    expenses: Record<string, Money>;
  };
  
  // ========== РќРћР’РћР• (Р‘Р»РѕРє 1: РРіСЂРѕРІРѕРµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ) ==========
  /** Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ payload СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ (РІСЃРµ РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ Р±РµР· С„СѓРЅРєС†РёР№). */
  getSavePayload: () => Record<string, unknown>;
  saveGame: () => void;
  loadGame: (saveData: any) => void;
}

      lastDealTimestamp: 0,

  // ========== Р¤РћРќР”РћР’Р«Р™ Р Р«РќРћРљ ==========
  buyShares: (companyId, shares) => {
    const s = get();
    const company = s.marketCompanies.find((c) => c.id === companyId);
    if (!company) return { ok: false, reason: 'РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°' };

    const existing = s.marketPositions.find((p) => p.companyId === companyId);
    const result = buyShares({
      cash: s.balanceDecimal,
      companyId,
      currentPrice: company.price,
      shares,
      existingPosition: existing,
      policy: TRADE_POLICY,
    });
    if (!result.ok) return { ok: false, reason: result.reason };

    const { result: trade } = result;
    const gross = trade.grossAmount;
    const commission = trade.commission;
    const total = moneyAdd(gross, commission);

    // РЎРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ РґРµРЅСЊРіРё (gross + commission) С‡РµСЂРµР· recordTransaction
    const tx = get().recordTransaction({
      direction: 'expense',
      amount: total,
      category: 'stock_buy',
      description: `РџРѕРєСѓРїРєР° Р°РєС†РёР№: ${company.name} Г— ${shares}`,
      relatedEntityId: companyId,
    });
    if (!tx.ok) return { ok: false, reason: tx.reason ?? 'РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СЃРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ' };

    // РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ РїРѕСЂС‚С„РµР»СЊ
    const newPositions = existing
      ? s.marketPositions.map((p) => (p.companyId === companyId ? trade.position! : p))
      : [...s.marketPositions, trade.position!];

    set({
      marketPositions: newPositions,
    });

    toast.success(`РљСѓРїР»РµРЅРѕ ${shares} Р°РєС†РёР№ ${company.name}`, {
      description: `РџРѕС‚СЂР°С‡РµРЅРѕ ${formatMoney(total)}`,
    });
    return { ok: true };
  },

  sellShares: (companyId, shares) => {
    const s = get();
    const company = s.marketCompanies.find((c) => c.id === companyId);
    if (!company) return { ok: false, reason: 'РљРѕРјРїР°РЅРёСЏ РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅР°' };
    const position = s.marketPositions.find((p) => p.companyId === companyId);
    if (!position) return { ok: false, reason: 'РќРµС‚ С‚Р°РєРѕР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё' };

    const result = sellShares({
      cash: s.balanceDecimal,
      companyId,
      currentPrice: company.price,
      shares,
      position,
      policy: TRADE_POLICY,
    });
    if (!result.ok) return { ok: false, reason: result.reason };

    const { result: trade } = result;
    const net = trade.cash;
    const proceeds = moneySub(net, s.balanceDecimal); // СЂР°Р·РЅРёС†Р°

    // РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РёС‚СЊ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІР° С‡РµСЂРµР· recordTransaction
    const tx = get().recordTransaction({
      direction: 'income',
      amount: proceeds,
      category: 'stock_sell',
      description: `РџСЂРѕРґР°Р¶Р° Р°РєС†РёР№: ${company.name} Г— ${shares}`,


########## PROCESSORS: processors.ts ##########

// (state, monthIndex) => state.

//   10 payroll          вЂ” Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹ СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ (Р‘Р»РѕРє 7)
//   20 businesses       вЂ” СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЌРєРѕРЅРѕРјРёРєР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ (Р‘Р»РѕРє 4)
//   30 credits          вЂ” РїР»Р°С‚РµР¶Рё РїРѕ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Р°Рј Рё РїСЂРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РєРё (Р‘Р»РѕРє 12)
//   40 market           вЂ” РґРёРІРёРґРµРЅРґС‹, РєСѓРїРѕРЅС‹, СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ СЂС‹РЅРєР° (Р‘Р»РѕРє 9)
//   50 events           вЂ” РєСЂСѓРїРЅС‹Рµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° (Р‘Р»РѕРє 8)
//   90 capital-snapshot вЂ” СЃРЅРёРјРѕРє РєР°РїРёС‚Р°Р»Р° РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР° (Р‘Р»РѕРє 3)
//
// Р—Р°РіР»СѓС€РєРё РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°СЋС‚ state Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№. РћРЅРё РЅСѓР¶РЅС‹, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РІ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёС…
// Р±Р»РѕРєР°С… РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹Р»Рѕ РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ С‚РµР»Рѕ, РЅРµ РјРµРЅСЏСЏ СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂСѓ.
