// TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 3): РІС‹РЅРµСЃС‚Рё СЌС‚Рё СЃРµР»РµРєС‚РѕСЂС‹ РІ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РјРѕРґСѓР»СЊ РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРµРґР°РІР°С‚СЊ
// С‡РµСЂРµР· Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ processors.ts РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРµР» РѕС‚ selectors.

/**
 * Р’СЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРѕСЂ РґРѕС…РѕРґР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ Рё РіРѕСЂРѕРґР° вЂ” С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚, С‡С‚Рѕ Р±С‹Р»
 * РІ onCompletedMonths РґРѕ СЂРµС„Р°РєС‚РѕСЂРёРЅРіР°. Р‘СѓРґРµС‚ СѓРґР°Р»С‘РЅ РІ Р‘Р»РѕРєРµ 4, РєРѕРіРґР°
 * РЅР°СЃС‚РѕСЏС‰Р°СЏ СЌРєРѕРЅРѕРјРёРєР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ (BusinessRuntime) Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Р№ РјРµС…Р°РЅРёР·Рј.
 */

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 7): Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹ СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ (staff/employeeEngine.ts) вЂ” РІС‹С‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ
 * РёР· Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ-СЃС‡РµС‚РѕРІ Рё РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅР°РІР»РёРІР°С‚СЊ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃС‹ РїСЂРё РЅРµС…РІР°С‚РєРµ.
 */
const payrollProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'payroll',
  order: 10,
  run: (state, month) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(state.employees) || state.employees.length === 0) {
      return state;
    }

    // 1. #10: РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј business.monthlyPayroll = С„Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹ Р·Р° РјРµСЃСЏС†.
    //    Р”РµРЅСЊРіРё Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РќР• СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј Р·РґРµСЃСЊ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ settleBusinessMonth
    //    (order 15), РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЏ payroll РІ operatingCosts. Р•РґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№
    //    РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЏ Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚ вЂ” businessesProcessor.
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];
    const updatedBusinesses = state.businesses.map((b) => {
      const payroll = getMonthlyPayroll(state.employees, b.id);
      if (payroll !== ZERO_MONEY) {
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-salary-${b.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `business:${b.id}`,
          direction: 'expense',
          category: 'salary',
          amount: payroll,
          description: `${b.name}: Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹`,
          relatedEntityId: b.id,
        });
      }
      return { ...b, monthlyPayroll: payroll };
    });

    // 2. РџСЂРѕРґРІРёРіР°РµРј СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ (РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ СЃС‚Р°Р¶Р°, СѓС…РѕРґС‹)
    const result = advanceEmployeesMonth(state.employees);

    // 3. РђРІС‚РѕР·Р°РјРµРЅР° РѕС‚ HR
    const nextEmployees: EmployeeRuntime[] = [...result.employees];
    for (const resigned of result.resigned) {
      if (resigned.role === 'hr') continue; // HR СЃР°Рј СЃРµР±СЏ РЅРµ Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚
      const hasHr = nextEmployees.some(
        (e) => e.businessId === resigned.businessId && e.role === 'hr',
      );
      if (!hasHr) continue;

      // HR РЅР°РЅРёРјР°РµС‚ Р·Р°РјРµРЅСѓ С‚РѕРіРѕ Р¶Рµ С‚РёРїР°
      const def = EMPLOYEE_ROLES_BY_ID[resigned.role];
      if (!def) continue;
      const salary = Math.round(
        def.salaryMin + Math.random() * (def.salaryMax - def.salaryMin),
      );
      const months = Math.round(
        def.contractMinMonths +
          Math.random() * (def.contractMaxMonths - def.contractMinMonths),
      );
      nextEmployees.push({
        id: `emp_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`,
        businessId: resigned.businessId,
        role: resigned.role,
        name: def.name,
        qualification: 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
        monthlySalary: String(salary),
        monthsEmployed: 0,
        monthsRemaining: months,
      });
      journal.push({
        id: `ledger-hire-${resigned.businessId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
        monthIndex: month,
        accountId: `business:${resigned.businessId}`,
        direction: 'expense',
        category: 'salary',
        amount: '0',
        description: `HR Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёР»: ${def.name}`,
        relatedEntityId: resigned.businessId,
      });
    }

    return {
      ...state,
      employees: nextEmployees,
      businesses: updatedBusinesses,
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 4): СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ СЌРєРѕРЅРѕРјРёРєР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ. Р—Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚ legacyIncomeProcessor.
 * Р РµР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ Р±СѓРґРµС‚ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚СЊ settleBusinessMonth() РёР·
 * game/business/operations.ts Рё consumeMonthlyResources() РёР·
 * game/logistics/inventory.ts.
 */
// ========== РџР РћРР—Р’РћР”РЎРўР’Рђ Р РћР¤РРЎРќР«Р• РљРћРњРџРђРќРР ==========
// order 14 вЂ” Р”Рћ businesses (15), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РїСЂРѕРґСѓРєС†РёРё Р±С‹Р»Рѕ С‡С‚Рѕ РїРѕС‚СЂРµР±Р»СЏС‚СЊ.
const enterpriseProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'enterprises',
  order: 14,
  run: (state, month) => {
    if (!state.enterprises || state.enterprises.length === 0) return state;

    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];
    const updatedEnterprises: EnterpriseRuntime[] = [];
        let monthlyNpcSold = { ...(state.monthlyNpcSold ?? {}) };

    // РљРѕРїРёСЏ СЃРєР»Р°РґРѕРІ
    const warehouses = (state.warehouses ?? []).map((w) => ({
      ...w,
      items: { ...w.items },
    }));

    for (const ent of state.enterprises) {
      if (ent.status === 'closed') {
        updatedEnterprises.push(ent);
        continue;
      }

      const isProduction = ent.type === 'production';
      const prodDef = isProduction ? PRODUCTIONS_BY_ID[ent.defId] : null;
      const offDef = !isProduction ? OFFICE_COMPANIES_BY_ID[ent.defId] : null;
      const def = prodDef ?? offDef;
      if (!def) {
        updatedEnterprises.push(ent);
        continue;
      }

      const updated: EnterpriseRuntime = {
        ...ent,
        inventory: { ...ent.inventory },
        // Defaults РґР»СЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹С… СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёР№
        accruedLiabilities: ent.accruedLiabilities ?? '0',
        monthsWithoutPayroll: ent.monthsWithoutPayroll ?? 0,
        monthsWithoutSupply: ent.monthsWithoutSupply ?? 0,
      };

      // в”Ђв”Ђ 1. Р—Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      // РЎРїРёСЃР°РЅРёРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РёР· РєР°СЃСЃС‹ РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёСЏ. РќРµС‚ РґРµРЅРµРі вЂ” РєРѕРїРёС‚СЃСЏ accruedLiabilities,
      // С‡РµСЂРµР· 3 РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° РЅРµСѓРїР»Р°С‚С‹ СѓРІРѕР»СЊРЅСЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ Р’РЎР•. РР· РєРѕС€РµР»СЊРєР° РёРіСЂРѕРєР° РЅРµ СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј.
      const payroll = getMonthlyPayroll(state.employees, ent.id);
      updated.monthlyPayroll = payroll;

      const cash = Number(updated.cashBalance);
      const payrollNum = Number(payroll);
      if (payrollNum > 0) {
        if (cash >= payrollNum) {
          updated.cashBalance = String(cash - payrollNum);
          updated.monthsWithoutPayroll = 0;
          // Р•СЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р»Рё РЅР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹Рµ РґРѕР»РіРё вЂ” СѓРјРµРЅСЊС€Р°РµРј РёС…
          if (Number(updated.accruedLiabilities) > 0) {
            updated.accruedLiabilities = String(
              Math.max(0, Number(updated.accruedLiabilities) - payrollNum),
            );
          }
        } else {
          const shortfall = payrollNum - cash;
          updated.cashBalance = '0';
          updated.accruedLiabilities = String(
            Number(updated.accruedLiabilities) + payrollNum,
          );
          updated.monthsWithoutPayroll = (updated.monthsWithoutPayroll ?? 0) + 1;

          journal.push({
            id: `ledger-salary-unpaid-${ent.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
            monthIndex: month,
            accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
            direction: 'expense',
            category: 'salary',
            amount: String(Math.round(payrollNum)),
            description: `${def.name}: Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚Р° РЅРµ РІС‹РїР»Р°С‡РµРЅР° (РґРѕР»Рі ${Math.round(shortfall)} в‚Ѕ, ${updated.monthsWithoutPayroll}/3 РјРµСЃ)`,
            relatedEntityId: ent.id,
          });

          if (updated.monthsWithoutPayroll >= 3) {
            updated.status = 'stopped';
            journal.push({
              id: `ledger-salary-fired-${ent.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
              monthIndex: month,
              accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
              direction: 'expense',
              category: 'salary',
              amount: '0',
              description: `${def.name}: РІСЃРµ СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРё СѓРІРѕР»РµРЅС‹ (3 РјРµСЃ Р±РµР· Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹)`,
              relatedEntityId: ent.id,
            });
          }
        }
      }

      // в”Ђв”Ђ 2. РџСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІРѕ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      if (isProduction && prodDef && updated.status === 'operating') {
        // 2a. РџРѕС‚СЂРµР±Р»СЏРµРј keyResources: РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ в†’ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРєР»Р°Рґ (Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ) в†’ NPC (Р·Р° РґРµРЅСЊРіРё).
        //     Р•СЃР»Рё NPC РЅРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ вЂ” РЅРµ РїРѕРєСѓРїР°РµРј, Р·Р°РїРѕРјРёРЅР°РµРј РґРµС„РёС†РёС‚. РР· РєРѕС€РµР»СЊРєР° РёРіСЂРѕРєР° РЅРµ СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј.
        const whIdxForEnt = updated.linkedWarehouseId
          ? warehouses.findIndex((w) => w.id === updated.linkedWarehouseId)
          : -1;

        let totalShortageRatio = 1; // РґР»СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ РІС‹РїСѓСЃРєР°
        let anyShortage = false;

        for (const [resId, required] of Object.entries(prodDef.keyResources)) {
          const have = updated.inventory[resId]?.quantity ?? 0;
          let remaining = required;
          let obtained = 0;

          // РЁР°Рі 1: СЃРІРѕР№ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ вЂ” Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ
          const fromInventory = Math.min(have, remaining);
          if (fromInventory > 0) {
            const left = have - fromInventory;
            if (left <= 0) delete updated.inventory[resId];
            else updated.inventory[resId] = { ...updated.inventory[resId], quantity: left };
            remaining -= fromInventory;
            obtained += fromInventory;
          }

          // РЁР°Рі 2: СЃРєР»Р°Рґ РёРіСЂРѕРєР° вЂ” Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ, С‚РѕРІР°СЂ РїРµСЂРµРєР»Р°РґС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёСЏ
          if (remaining > 0 && whIdxForEnt >= 0) {
            const wh = warehouses[whIdxForEnt];
            if (wh && !wh.activeEvent) {
              const draw = withdrawFromWarehouse(
                wh as unknown as Warehouse,
                resId,
                remaining,
              );
              if (draw.ok && draw.withdrawn > 0) {
                // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЃРєР»Р°Рґ (withdrawFromWarehouse РІРµСЂРЅСѓР» РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РѕР±СЉРµРєС‚)
                warehouses[whIdxForEnt] = {
                  ...wh,
                  items: draw.warehouse.items,
                };
                // РљР»Р°РґС‘Рј РІ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёСЏ СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ СЃРєР»Р°РґР°
                const existing = updated.inventory[resId];
                if (existing) {
                  const newQty = existing.quantity + draw.withdrawn;
                  const oldVal = Number(existing.averageUnitCost) * existing.quantity;
                  const addVal = draw.unitCost * draw.withdrawn;
                  updated.inventory[resId] = {
                    quantity: newQty,
                    averageUnitCost: String((oldVal + addVal) / newQty),
                  };
                } else {
                  updated.inventory[resId] = {
                    quantity: draw.withdrawn,
                    averageUnitCost: String(draw.unitCost),
                  };
                }
                // РЎСЂР°Р·Сѓ СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј РёР· РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЏ (СЌС‚Рѕ СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°)
                const left = updated.inventory[resId].quantity - draw.withdrawn;
                if (left <= 0) delete updated.inventory[resId];
                else updated.inventory[resId] = { ...updated.inventory[resId], quantity: left };
                remaining -= draw.withdrawn;
                obtained += draw.withdrawn;

                journal.push({
                  id: `ledger-internal-transfer-${ent.id}-${resId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
                  monthIndex: month,
                  accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
                  direction: 'income',
                  category: 'internal_transfer',
                  amount: '0',
                  description: `${def.name}: СЃРѕ СЃРєР»Р°РґР° ${draw.withdrawn} Г— ${RESOURCES_BY_ID[resId]?.name ?? resId} (СѓС‡С‘С‚ ${Math.round(draw.totalCost)} в‚Ѕ)`,
                  relatedEntityId: ent.id,
                });
              }
            }
          }

          // РЁР°Рі 3: NPC вЂ” Р·Р° РґРµРЅСЊРіРё СЃРѕ СЃРІРѕРµР№ РєР°СЃСЃС‹
          if (remaining > 0) {
            const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[resId];
            const priceMult = getResourcePriceMultiplier(state.activeEvents ?? [], resId, month);
            const unitPrice = Number(res?.basePrice ?? 0) * 0.7 * priceMult;
            const cost = unitPrice * remaining;
            const entCash = Number(updated.cashBalance);
            if (entCash >= cost) {
              updated.cashBalance = String(entCash - cost);
              // Р’ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ вЂ” Рё СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ СЃРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ (СЌС‚Рѕ СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°)
              const existing = updated.inventory[resId];
              if (existing) {
                const newQty = existing.quantity + remaining;
                const oldVal = Number(existing.averageUnitCost) * existing.quantity;
                const addVal = unitPrice * remaining;
                updated.inventory[resId] = {
                  quantity: newQty,
                  averageUnitCost: String((oldVal + addVal) / newQty),
                };
              } else {
                updated.inventory[resId] = {
                  quantity: remaining,
                  averageUnitCost: String(unitPrice),
                };
              }
              const left = updated.inventory[resId].quantity - remaining;
              if (left <= 0) delete updated.inventory[resId];
              else updated.inventory[resId] = { ...updated.inventory[resId], quantity: left };
              obtained += remaining;
              remaining = 0;

              journal.push({
                id: `ledger-npc-purchase-${ent.id}-${resId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
                monthIndex: month,
                accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
                direction: 'expense',
                category: 'enterprise_purchase',
                amount: String(Math.round(cost)),
                description: `${def.name}: Р·Р°РєСѓРїРєР° ${remaining + obtained} Г— ${res?.name ?? resId} Сѓ NPC`,
                relatedEntityId: ent.id,
              });
            } else {
              // РќРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ РґРµРЅРµРі вЂ” РЅРµ РїРѕРєСѓРїР°РµРј. Р—Р°РїРѕРјРёРЅР°РµРј РґРµС„РёС†РёС‚.
              anyShortage = true;
              const gotRatio = obtained / required;
              if (gotRatio < totalShortageRatio) totalShortageRatio = gotRatio;
              journal.push({
                id: `ledger-shortage-${ent.id}-${resId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
                monthIndex: month,
                accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
                direction: 'expense',
                category: 'enterprise_purchase',
                amount: '0',
                description: `${def.name}: РЅРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ ${Math.round(cost)} в‚Ѕ РЅР° ${res?.name ?? resId} (РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ ${remaining})`,
                relatedEntityId: ent.id,
              });
            }
          }
        }

        updated.monthsWithoutSupply = anyShortage
          ? (updated.monthsWithoutSupply ?? 0) + 1
          : 0;

        // 2b. РџСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґРёРј РїСЂРѕРґСѓРєС†РёСЋ РІ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ
        if (updated.status === 'operating') {
          for (const [resId, qty] of Object.entries(prodDef.produces)) {
                        // Р§Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РІС‹РїСѓСЃРє: РµСЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р» РґРµС„РёС†РёС‚ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґРёРј РїСЂРѕРїРѕСЂС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ.
            const partialMult = anyShortage ? totalShortageRatio : 1;
            const produced = Math.floor(qty * updated.level * partialMult);
            const existing = updated.inventory[resId];
            if (existing) {
              updated.inventory[resId] = {
                ...existing,
                quantity: existing.quantity + produced,
              };
            } else {
              const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[resId];
              updated.inventory[resId] = {
                quantity: produced,
                averageUnitCost: String(res?.basePrice ?? 0),
              };
            }
          }

          // 2c. Р Р°СЃРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ
          const whIdx = updated.linkedWarehouseId
            ? warehouses.findIndex((w) => w.id === updated.linkedWarehouseId)
            : -1;

          for (const resId of Object.keys(prodDef.produces)) {
            const total = updated.inventory[resId]?.quantity ?? 0;
            if (total <= 0) continue;

            const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[resId];
            const basePrice = Number(res?.basePrice ?? 0);
            const dist = updated.productionDistribution?.[resId];

            let toWh = 0;
            let toSell = 0;

            if (whIdx < 0) {
              // РќРµС‚ СЃРєР»Р°РґР° в†’ РІСЃС‘ РЅР° РїСЂРѕРґР°Р¶Сѓ (Г—0.7)
              toSell = total;
            } else if (dist) {
              toWh = Math.min(dist.toWarehouse, total);
              toSell = Math.min(dist.toSell, total - toWh);
            } else {
              // РЎРєР»Р°Рґ РµСЃС‚СЊ, РЅРѕ РїРѕР»Р·СѓРЅРєРё РЅРµ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРµРЅС‹ в†’ РІСЃС‘ РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°Рґ
              toWh = total;
            }

            const unsold = total - toWh - toSell;

            // Р—Р°С‡РёСЃР»СЏРµРј РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°Рґ
            if (toWh > 0 && whIdx >= 0) {
              const wh = warehouses[whIdx];
              const existing = wh.items[resId];
              if (existing) {
                wh.items[resId] = { ...existing, quantity: existing.quantity + toWh };
              } else {
                wh.items[resId] = {
                  resourceId: resId,
                  quantity: toWh,
                  averageUnitCost: String(basePrice),
                };
              }
            }

            // в”Ђв”Ђ РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° NPC в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
            // РћРґРёРЅ РїРѕРєСѓРїР°С‚РµР»СЊ: toSell (Г—0.7) Рё unsold (Г—0.4) РґРµР»СЏС‚ РѕРґРёРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚.
            const remaining = getRemainingNpcDemand(monthlyNpcSold, resId);
            const actualToSell = Math.min(toSell, remaining);
            const remainingAfterSell = remaining - actualToSell;
            const actualUnsold = Math.min(unsold, remainingAfterSell);

            const wantedToSell = toSell + unsold;
            const actuallySold = actualToSell + actualUnsold;
            const leftInInventory = wantedToSell - actuallySold;

            if (actuallySold > 0) {
              const revenue =
                actualToSell * basePrice * 0.7 +
                actualUnsold * basePrice * 0.4;
              updated.cashBalance = String(Number(updated.cashBalance) + revenue);
              monthlyNpcSold[resId] = (monthlyNpcSold[resId] ?? 0) + actuallySold;
            }

            // РќРµРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅРЅРѕРµ РёР·-Р·Р° Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРµ РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёСЏ
            if (leftInInventory > 0) {
              updated.inventory[resId] = {
                quantity: leftInInventory,
                averageUnitCost: String(basePrice),
              };
            } else {
              delete updated.inventory[resId];
            }

            if (leftInInventory > 0) {
              journal.push({
                id: `ledger-npc-limit-${ent.id}-${resId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
                monthIndex: month,
                accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
                direction: 'expense',
                category: 'enterprise_purchase',
                amount: '0',
                description: `${def.name}: СЃРїСЂРѕСЃ NPC РЅР° В«${res?.name ?? resId}В» РёСЃС‡РµСЂРїР°РЅ, ${leftInInventory} РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРµ`,
                relatedEntityId: ent.id,
              });
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // в”Ђв”Ђ 3. Р’С‹СЂСѓС‡РєР°, СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґС‹, РЅР°Р»РѕРі в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      // Payroll СѓР¶Рµ СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅ РІ С€Р°РіРµ 1 вЂ” Р·РґРµСЃСЊ РµРіРѕ РќР• РІС‹С‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕ.
      const grossRevenue = Number(def.baseRevenue) * updated.level * updated.demandMultiplier;
      const monthlyExpenses =
        Number(updated.monthlyRent) +
        Number(updated.monthlyUtilities) +
        Number(updated.monthlyMaintenance);
      const profitBeforeTax = Math.max(0, grossRevenue - monthlyExpenses);
      const tax = profitBeforeTax * updated.taxRate;
      const netFlow = grossRevenue - monthlyExpenses - tax;

      const newCash = Number(updated.cashBalance) + netFlow;
      updated.cashBalance = String(Math.max(0, newCash));
      if (newCash < 0 && updated.status === 'operating') {
        updated.status = 'stopped';
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-ent-stop-${ent.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
          direction: 'expense',
          category: 'enterprise_cost',
          amount: '0',
          description: `${def.name}: РѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРѕ (РєР°СЃСЃР° СѓС€Р»Р° РІ РјРёРЅСѓСЃ)`,
          relatedEntityId: ent.id,
        });
      }

      if (grossRevenue > 0) {
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-ent-revenue-${ent.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `enterprise:${ent.id}`,
          direction: 'income',
          category: 'enterprise_income',
          amount: String(Math.round(grossRevenue)),
          description: `${def.name}: РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєР°`,
          relatedEntityId: ent.id,
        });
      }

      updatedEnterprises.push(updated);
    }
    // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРѕРІ РїСЂРµРґРїСЂРёСЏС‚РёР№, Сѓ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹С… 3+ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° РЅРµС‚ Р·Р°СЂРїР»Р°С‚С‹
    const firedEnterpriseIds = new Set(
      updatedEnterprises
        .filter((e) => e.status === 'stopped' && (e.monthsWithoutPayroll ?? 0) >= 3)
        .map((e) => e.id),
    );
    const nextEmployees =
      firedEnterpriseIds.size > 0
        ? (state.employees ?? []).filter((e) => !firedEnterpriseIds.has(e.businessId))
        : state.employees;

    return {
      ...state,
      enterprises: updatedEnterprises,
      employees: nextEmployees,
      warehouses,
      monthlyNpcSold,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};
const competitorProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'competitors',
  order: 12,
  run: (state, month) => {
    const existing = state.competitors ?? [];

    // 1. РђРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРЅРєСѓСЂРµРЅС‚С‹ РјРµРЅСЏСЋС‚ С†РµРЅС‹/СЂРµРїСѓС‚Р°С†РёСЋ/РґРѕР»СЋ
    let updated = existing.map((c) => simulateCompetitorMonth(c));

    // 2. РђРєС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ РЅРѕРІС‹С… (РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ РјРµСЃС‚Рѕ Рё СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ РїРѕР·РІРѕР»СЏРµС‚)
    const playerSectors = [
      ...new Set(
        (state.businesses ?? [])
          .map((b) => b.sectorId)
          .filter(Boolean),
      ),
    ];

    // #19: РµРґРёРЅС‹Р№ РёСЃС‚РѕС‡РЅРёРє РїСЂР°РІРґС‹ вЂ” getCompanyLevel (РєР°Рє РІ UI Рё СЂРµР№С‚РёРЅРіРµ).
    // Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ Р±С‹Р»Р° РІС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ С„РѕСЂРјСѓР»Р° Math.floor(newLevels/5)+1, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ
    // СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґРёР»Р°СЃСЊ СЃ UI РЅР° 1-2 СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ РїСЂРё 5+ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃР°С….
    const effectiveLevel = getCompanyLevel(state);
    if (updated.length < MAX_ACTIVE_COMPETITORS) {
      const fresh = activateCompetitors(
        state.marketCompanies ?? [],
        updated,
        playerSectors,
        effectiveLevel,
        month,
      );

      if (fresh.length > 0) {
        updated = [...updated, ...fresh];

        // РћС‚РјРµС‚РёС‚СЊ РІ marketCompanies С„Р»Р°Рі isActiveCompetitor
        const freshIds = new Set(fresh.map((c) => c.companyId));
        const nextCompanies = (state.marketCompanies ?? []).map((c) =>
          freshIds.has(c.id) ? { ...c, isActiveCompetitor: true } : c,
        );

        return { ...state, competitors: updated, marketCompanies: nextCompanies };
      }
    }

    return { ...state, competitors: updated };
  },
};
// ========== C: РЎРћР‘Р«РўРРЇ РЎРљР›РђР”РћР’ ==========
// Р Р°Р· РІ 6 РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ, 60% С€Р°РЅСЃ. РЈРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёР№ СЂРµС€Р°РµС‚ СЃР°Рј (СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ РѕС‚РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ).
// Р‘РµР· СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РµРіРѕ вЂ” СЃРєР»Р°Рґ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ, РёРіСЂРѕРє РїР»Р°С‚РёС‚ Р·Р° СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ.
const warehouseProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'warehouse-events',
  order: 16,
  run: (state, month) => {
    if (!state.warehouses || state.warehouses.length === 0) return state;

    let news = state.news ?? [];

    const updatedWarehouses = state.warehouses.map((wh) => {
      // РЈР¶Рµ РµСЃС‚СЊ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ в†’ Р¶РґС‘Рј РёРіСЂРѕРєР°
      if (wh.activeEvent && !wh.activeEvent.resolved) return wh;

      // Р Р°Р· РІ 6 РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ (РјРµСЃСЏС† 0, 6, 12, ...)
      if (month === 0 || month % 6 !== 0) return wh;

      // 60% С€Р°РЅСЃ
      if (Math.random() > 0.6) return wh;

      // РЈРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёР№ СЂРµС€Р°РµС‚ СЃР°Рј
      const hasManager = wh.employees.some((e) => e.role === 'manager');
      if (hasManager) return wh;

      const hasStorekeeper = wh.employees.some((e) => e.role === 'storekeeper');
      const hasLoader = wh.employees.some((e) => e.role === 'loader');

      // РџРѕР»РѕРјРєР° РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅР° С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё СЃРєР»Р°Рґ СЃС‚Р°СЂС€Рµ 6 РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ
      const isOldEnough =
        wh.createdAtMonth === undefined || month - wh.createdAtMonth >= 6;

      const kinds: Array<'theft' | 'fire' | 'pipe_burst' | 'equipment_break'> = [
        'theft', 'fire', 'pipe_burst',
      ];
      if (isOldEnough) kinds.push('equipment_break');

      const kind = kinds[Math.floor(Math.random() * kinds.length)];
      const size = wh.size;

      // в”Ђв”Ђ РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј СѓС‰РµСЂР± в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      const newItems = { ...wh.items };
      let cost = 0;

      if (kind === 'theft') {
        const percentMin = hasStorekeeper ? 0.03 : 0.05;
        const percentMax = hasStorekeeper ? 0.10 : 0.15;
        const percent = percentMin + Math.random() * (percentMax - percentMin);
        const resIds = Object.keys(newItems).filter((id) => newItems[id].quantity > 0);
        if (resIds.length > 0) {
          const resId = resIds[Math.floor(Math.random() * resIds.length)];
          const lost = Math.ceil(newItems[resId].quantity * percent);
          newItems[resId] = {
            ...newItems[resId],
            quantity: Math.max(0, newItems[resId].quantity - lost),
          };
          if (newItems[resId].quantity === 0) delete newItems[resId];
        }
        const repairCosts = { small: 20_000, medium: 30_000, large: 50_000, huge: 80_000 };
        cost = repairCosts[size];
      } else if (kind === 'pipe_burst') {
        const percentMin = hasStorekeeper ? 0.01 : 0.05;
        const percentMax = hasStorekeeper ? 0.10 : 0.15;
        const percent = percentMin + Math.random() * (percentMax - percentMin);
        for (const resId of Object.keys(newItems)) {
          const lost = Math.ceil(newItems[resId].quantity * percent);
          newItems[resId] = {
            ...newItems[resId],
            quantity: Math.max(0, newItems[resId].quantity - lost),
          };
          if (newItems[resId].quantity === 0) delete newItems[resId];
        }
        const repairCosts = { small: 30_000, medium: 50_000, large: 70_000, huge: 100_000 };
        cost = repairCosts[size];
      } else if (kind === 'fire') {
        const percentMin = hasStorekeeper ? 0.05 : 0.10;
        const percentMax = hasStorekeeper ? 0.15 : 0.30;
        const percent = percentMin + Math.random() * (percentMax - percentMin);
        for (const resId of Object.keys(newItems)) {
          const lost = Math.ceil(newItems[resId].quantity * percent);
          newItems[resId] = {
            ...newItems[resId],
            quantity: Math.max(0, newItems[resId].quantity - lost),
          };
          if (newItems[resId].quantity === 0) delete newItems[resId];
        }
        const repairCosts = { small: 50_000, medium: 80_000, large: 120_000, huge: 200_000 };
        cost = repairCosts[size];
      } else if (kind === 'equipment_break') {
        let repairCost = 20_000 + Math.random() * 80_000;
        if (hasLoader) repairCost *= 0.9;
        if (hasStorekeeper) repairCost *= 0.9;
        cost = Math.round(repairCost);
      }

      const event: WarehouseEvent = {
        kind,
        month,
        cost: Math.round(cost),
        resolved: false,
      };

      const eventNames: Record<string, string> = {
        theft: 'РљСЂР°Р¶Р°',
        fire: 'РџРѕР¶Р°СЂ',
        pipe_burst: 'РџСЂРѕСЂС‹РІ С‚СЂСѓР±С‹',
        equipment_break: 'РџРѕР»РѕРјРєР° РѕР±РѕСЂСѓРґРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ',
      };

      news = [
        {
          id: `news_wh_event_${wh.id}_${month}_${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          title: `вљ пёЏ ${eventNames[kind]} РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°РґРµ В«${wh.customName}В»`,
          body: `РЎРєР»Р°Рґ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ. РўСЂРµР±СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ СѓСЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ Р·Р° ${Math.round(cost).toLocaleString('ru-RU')} в‚Ѕ.`,
          category: 'event',
        },
        ...news,
      ].slice(0, 200);

      return {
        ...wh,
        items: newItems,
        activeEvent: event,
        lastEventMonth: month,
      };
    });

    return {
      ...state,
      warehouses: updatedWarehouses,
      news,
    };
  },
};

const businessesProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'businesses',
  order: 15,
  run: (state, month) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(state.businesses) || state.businesses.length === 0) {
      return state;
    }

    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];
    const updatedBusinesses: BusinessRuntime[] = [];
    const dismissBusinessIds = new Set<string>();

    // Р‘РѕРЅСѓСЃС‹ РѕС‚ РѕС„РёСЃРЅС‹С… РєРѕРјРїР°РЅРёР№
    const officeBonuses = getOfficeBonuses(state);
    const taxMultiplier = 1 - officeBonuses.taxReduction;
    const globalDemandBonus = 1 + officeBonuses.demandBonus;

    let warehouses: WarehouseRuntime[] = (state.warehouses ?? []).map((w) => ({
      ...w,
      items: { ...w.items },
      reserved: { ...w.reserved },
    }));

    for (const business of state.businesses) {
      const def = findDefForRuntime(business, BUSINESSES);
      if (!def) {
        updatedBusinesses.push(business);
        continue;
      }

      const caps = getAutomationCapabilities(state.employees, business.id);
      const efficiency = getEmployeeEfficiency(state.employees, business.id);

      // в”Ђв”Ђ A.4: С€С‚СЂР°С„ Р·Р° РЅРµС…РІР°С‚РєСѓ СЂР°Р±РѕС‡РµРіРѕ РїРµСЂСЃРѕРЅР°Р»Р° в”Ђв”Ђ
      // Р›РёРЅРµР№РЅР°СЏ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ: 0% С€С‚Р°С‚Р° в†’ Г—0 (РЅРµС‚ РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєРё), 100% в†’ Г—1.
      const workerStaff = def.workerStaff ?? {};
      let totalRequired = 0;
      let totalHired = 0;
      for (const [roleId, required] of Object.entries(workerStaff)) {
        totalRequired += required;
        const hired = state.employees.filter(
          (e) => e.businessId === business.id && e.role === roleId,
        ).length;
        totalHired += Math.min(hired, required);
      }
      const staffingMultiplier =
        totalRequired > 0 ? totalHired / totalRequired : 1;

      const sectorDemand = getSectorDemandMultiplier(
        state.activeEvents ?? [],
        business.sectorId,
        month,
      );

      const playerReputation = getTransportReputation(state) || 50;
      const competitorDemand = estimateDemandMultiplier({
        playerPriceMultiplier: 1,
        playerReputation,
        competitors: state.competitors ?? [],
        sectorId: business.sectorId,
      });

      const requirements = resourceRequirementsFromDef(def);
      let supplied = false;
      let consumedResourceCost: Money = ZERO_MONEY;
      let whIdx = -1;

      // в”Ђв”Ђ РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚РѕРІР°СЏ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРєР°: РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РјРµСЃСЏС† РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ в”Ђв”Ђ
      const isInitialMonth =
        business.initialSuppliedMonth !== undefined &&
        month === business.initialSuppliedMonth;

      if (isInitialMonth) {
        supplied = true;
        consumedResourceCost = ZERO_MONEY;
      } else {
        // в”Ђв”Ђ РќР°Р№С‚Рё РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃРєР»Р°Рґ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
        whIdx = business.linkedWarehouseId
          ? warehouses.findIndex((w) => w.id === business.linkedWarehouseId)
          : -1;
        const wh = whIdx >= 0 ? warehouses[whIdx] : null;

        // C.2.3: РµСЃР»Рё СЃРєР»Р°Рґ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµРј вЂ” Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РєР°Рє В«Р±РµР· СЃРєР»Р°РґР°В»
        const isWarehouseBlocked =
          wh && wh.activeEvent && !wh.activeEvent.resolved;

        if (wh && !isWarehouseBlocked) {
          // РџРµСЂРµСЃС‡С‘С‚ СЂРµР·РµСЂРІР°
          let reservedForBiz = 0;
          for (const req of requirements) {
            reservedForBiz += req.unitsPerLevel * business.level;
          }
          warehouses[whIdx].reserved = {
            ...warehouses[whIdx].reserved,
            [business.id]: reservedForBiz,
          };

          // в”Ђв”Ђ РђРІС‚РѕР·Р°РєСѓРїРєР°, РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ manager в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
          if (caps.autoManageOperations && business.status === 'operating') {
            const discount = wh.hasTrucks ? 0.5 : 0.7;

            for (const req of requirements) {
              const required = req.unitsPerLevel * business.level;
              const onWh = warehouses[whIdx].items[req.resourceId]?.quantity ?? 0;
              const need = Math.max(0, required - onWh);
              if (need <= 0) continue;

              const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[req.resourceId];
              const priceMult = getResourcePriceMultiplier(state.activeEvents ?? [], req.resourceId, month);
              const unitPrice = Number(res?.basePrice ?? 0) * discount * priceMult;
              const cost = unitPrice * need;

              const cash = Number(business.cashBalance ?? 0);
              if (cash >= cost) {
                business.cashBalance = String(cash - cost);
              } else {
                // РќРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ РЅР° NPC вЂ” РЅРµ РїРѕРєСѓРїР°РµРј, РѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РєР°Рє РґРµС„РёС†РёС‚.
                // РР· РєРѕС€РµР»СЊРєР° РёРіСЂРѕРєР° РќР• СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј.
                journal.push({
                  id: `ledger-biz-shortage-${business.id}-${req.resourceId}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
                  monthIndex: month,
                  accountId: `business:${business.id}`,
                  direction: 'expense',
                  category: 'business_purchase',
                  amount: '0',
                  description: `${business.name}: РЅРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ ${Math.round(cost)} в‚Ѕ РЅР° ${res?.name ?? req.resourceId} (РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ ${need})`,
                  relatedEntityId: business.id,
                });
                continue; // РЅРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°Рґ, РЅРµ СЃРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј РґРµРЅСЊРіРё
              }
              const existing = warehouses[whIdx].items[req.resourceId];
              if (existing) {
                const newQty = existing.quantity + need;
                const oldCost = Number(existing.averageUnitCost) * existing.quantity;
                const addCost = unitPrice * need;
                warehouses[whIdx].items[req.resourceId] = {
                  ...existing,
                  quantity: newQty,
                  averageUnitCost: String((oldCost + addCost) / newQty),
                };
              } else {
                warehouses[whIdx].items[req.resourceId] = {
                  resourceId: req.resourceId,
                  quantity: need,
                  averageUnitCost: String(unitPrice),
                };
              }
            }
          }

          // в”Ђв”Ђ РџРѕС‚СЂРµР±Р»РµРЅРёРµ РёР· СЃРєР»Р°РґР° в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
          const consumption = consumeMonthlyResources(
            wh as unknown as Warehouse,
            requirements,
            business.level,
          );
          warehouses[whIdx].items = consumption.warehouse.items as any;
          supplied = consumption.supplied;
          consumedResourceCost = consumption.consumedValue;
        } else if (business.inventory && Object.keys(business.inventory).length > 0) {
          const pseudoWarehouse: Warehouse = {
            capacity: 1_000_000,
            items: business.inventory as any,
          };
          const consumption = consumeMonthlyResources(
            pseudoWarehouse,
            requirements,
            business.level,
          );
          business.inventory = consumption.warehouse.items as any;
          supplied = consumption.supplied;
          consumedResourceCost = consumption.consumedValue;
        }
      }

      // в”Ђв”Ђ Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      // Р‘РѕРЅСѓСЃС‹: IT Рё СЂР°Р·РІР»РµРєР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°СЋС‚ РґРѕРї. РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєСѓ
      let sectorRevenueBonus = 1;
      if (business.sectorId === 'it') {
        sectorRevenueBonus *= 1 + officeBonuses.itRevenue;
      }
      if (business.id === 'cinema' || business.id === 'games') {
        sectorRevenueBonus *= 1 + officeBonuses.entertainmentRevenue;
      }

      const settlement = settleBusinessMonth(
        {
          ...business,
          demandMultiplier:
            business.demandMultiplier * sectorDemand * competitorDemand *
            globalDemandBonus * sectorRevenueBonus * staffingMultiplier,
          productivityMultiplier: efficiency,
          taxRate: business.taxRate * taxMultiplier,
        },
        {
          supplied,
          consumedResourceCost,
          eventMultiplier: 1,
        },
      );

      // в”Ђв”Ђ B: Р±СѓС…РіР°Р»С‚РµСЂ вЂ” РІР»РёСЏРµС‚ РЅР° compliance (РїРѕРєР° Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєР°) в”Ђв”Ђ
      // Р‘РѕРЅСѓСЃ +5вЂ“30% Рє РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєРµ СѓР±СЂР°РЅ (СЃРј. Р¤Р°Р·Р° 6). Р’ Р±СѓРґСѓС‰РµРј:
      // - СЃРЅРёР¶Р°РµС‚ СЂРёСЃРє С€С‚СЂР°С„Р° РїСЂРё РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРµ
      // - РѕР±СЃР»СѓР¶РёРІР°РµС‚ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅРЅРѕРµ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ
      const hasAccountant = state.employees.some(
        (e) => e.businessId === business.id && e.role === 'accountant',
      );
      settlement.business.compliance = hasAccountant ? 0.95 : 0.5;
      updatedBusinesses.push(settlement.business);

      // #17: 3 РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏ в†’ СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРё СѓРІРѕР»СЊРЅСЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ
      if (settlement.shouldDismissStaff && business.status !== 'closed') {
        dismissBusinessIds.add(business.id);
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-dismiss-${business.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `business:${business.id}`,
          direction: 'expense',
          category: 'salary',
          amount: '0',
          description: `${business.name}: СЃРѕС‚СЂСѓРґРЅРёРєРё СѓРІРѕР»РµРЅС‹ (3 РјРµСЃ РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏ)`,
          relatedEntityId: business.id,
        });
      }

      // в”Ђв”Ђ РџСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІРѕ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ СЃРєР»Р°Рґ) в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      if (def.produces && settlement.business.status !== 'stopped' && whIdx >= 0) {
        const whForProd = warehouses[whIdx];
        for (const [outputId, outputPerLevel] of Object.entries(def.produces)) {
          const totalOutput = Math.floor(outputPerLevel * business.level);
          if (totalOutput <= 0) continue;

          const usedNow = Object.values(whForProd.items).reduce(
            (sum, item) => sum + item.quantity,
            0,
          );
          const reservedTotal = Object.values(whForProd.reserved).reduce(
            (sum, v) => sum + v,
            0,
          );
          const effectiveCapacity = whForProd.capacity;
          const free = effectiveCapacity - usedNow - reservedTotal;
          if (totalOutput > free) continue;

          const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[outputId]?.basePrice ?? 0;
          const existing = whForProd.items[outputId];
          if (existing) {
            const newQty = existing.quantity + totalOutput;
            const oldCost = Number(existing.averageUnitCost) * existing.quantity;
            const addCost = res * totalOutput;
            whForProd.items[outputId] = {
              ...existing,
              quantity: newQty,
              averageUnitCost: String((oldCost + addCost) / newQty),
            };
          } else {
            whForProd.items[outputId] = {
              resourceId: outputId,
              quantity: totalOutput,
              averageUnitCost: String(res),
            };
          }
        }
      }

      // в”Ђв”Ђ Р–СѓСЂРЅР°Р» в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      if (settlement.revenue !== '0' && settlement.revenue !== ZERO_MONEY) {
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-revenue-${business.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `business:${business.id}`,
          direction: 'income',
          category: 'business_income',
          amount: settlement.revenue,
          description: `${business.name}: РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєР°`,
          relatedEntityId: business.id,
        });
      }

      const totalCost = moneyAdd(settlement.operatingCosts, settlement.tax);
      if (totalCost !== '0' && totalCost !== ZERO_MONEY) {
        journal.push({
          id: `ledger-cost-${business.id}-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: `business:${business.id}`,
          direction: 'expense',
          category: 'business_cost',
          amount: totalCost,
          description: `${business.name}: СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґС‹`,
          relatedEntityId: business.id,
        });
      }
    }

    const nextEmployees =
      dismissBusinessIds.size > 0
        ? state.employees.filter((e) => !dismissBusinessIds.has(e.businessId))
        : state.employees;

    return {
      ...state,
      businesses: updatedBusinesses,
      employees: nextEmployees,
      warehouses,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};
// ========== Р‘Р›РћРљ 14: РќР°Р»РѕРіРѕРІС‹Рµ РїРѕСЃС‚СѓРїР»РµРЅРёСЏ РІ РєР°Р·РЅСѓ РіРѕСЂРѕРґР° ==========
// 1 РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕР№ РјРµСЃСЏС† = 600 СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ (GDD: 10 РјРёРЅСѓС‚ РЅР° x1).
// getCityIncomePerSec СѓР¶Рµ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ в‚Ѕ/СЃРµРє, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СѓРјРЅРѕР¶Р°РµРј РЅР° 600.
// Р­С‚Рѕ С‚Р° Р¶Рµ С„РѕСЂРјСѓР»Р°, С‡С‚Рѕ РІРёРґРёС‚ РёРіСЂРѕРє РІ UI вЂ” РґРѕС…РѕРґ РіРѕСЂРѕРґР° РЅРµ СЂР°СЃС…РѕРґРёС‚СЃСЏ СЃ
// РѕС‚РѕР±СЂР°Р¶РµРЅРёРµРј.
const GAME_MONTH_SECONDS = 600;

const cityTreasuryProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'city-treasury',
  order: 25, // РјРµР¶РґСѓ businesses (15) Рё credits (30): РґРѕС…РѕРґ СЃРѕР±СЂР°РЅ в†’ РґРѕР»РіРё СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅС‹
  run: (state, _month) => {
    const perSecond = getCityIncomePerSec(state);
    const monthly = Math.floor(perSecond * GAME_MONTH_SECONDS);
    if (monthly <= 0) return state;

    return {
      ...state,
      city: {
        ...state.city,
        treasury: state.city.treasury + monthly,
      },
    };
  },
};
// ========== РђР Р•РќР”Рђ РќР•Р”Р’РР–РРњРћРЎРўР Р Р–РљРҐ РЎРљР›РђР”РћР’ ==========
const rentalProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'rental',
  order: 28, // РџРѕСЃР»Рµ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃРѕРІ (15), РЅРѕ РґРѕ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚РѕРІ (30)
  run: (state, month) => {
    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];
    const nextRented: Record<string, any> = {};

    // 1. РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёРµ Р°СЂРµРЅРґРЅРѕР№ РїР»Р°С‚С‹
    for (const [id, rental] of Object.entries(state.rentedProperties ?? {})) {
      const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
        balance: balanceDecimal,
        amount: String(rental.rentPrice),
        direction: 'income',
        monthIndex: month,
        accountId: 'player_main',
        category: 'rental_income',
        description: `РђСЂРµРЅРґР°: ${rental.tenantName} (${id})`,
        relatedEntityId: id,
      });
      if (tx.ok) {
        balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
        journal.push(tx.entry);
      }

      // РЈРјРµРЅСЊС€Р°РµРј СЃСЂРѕРє Р°СЂРµРЅРґС‹
      const monthsLeft = rental.monthsLeft - 1;
      if (monthsLeft > 0) {
        nextRented[id] = { ...rental, monthsLeft };
      }
      // Р•СЃР»Рё monthsLeft === 0 вЂ” Р°СЂРµРЅРґР°С‚РѕСЂ СЃСЉРµС…Р°Р», РІ nextRented РЅРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј.
      // РЎРєР»Р°Рґ/РєРІР°СЂС‚РёСЂР° СЃРЅРѕРІР° РїСѓСЃС‚СѓРµС‚, Р–РљРҐ СЃРЅРѕРІР° РїР»Р°С‚РёС‚ РёРіСЂРѕРє.
    }

    // 2. РћРїР»Р°С‚Р° Р–РљРҐ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅС‹С… СЃРєР»Р°РґРѕРІ РІ СЂРµР¶РёРјРµ "Р›РёС‡РЅРѕРµ РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёРµ")
    const ownedWarehouses = (state.ownedProperty ?? []).filter((id) => {
      const def = REAL_ESTATE.find((r) => r.id === id);
      return def?.category === 'warehouse';
    });

    for (const whId of ownedWarehouses) {
      const mode = state.propertyMode[whId] ?? 'personal';
      if (mode === 'rent') continue; // Р•СЃР»Рё СЃРґР°РЅ вЂ” Р–РљРҐ РЅРµ РїР»Р°С‚РёРј

      const def = REAL_ESTATE.find((r) => r.id === whId);
      if (!def) continue;

      // Р–РљРҐ = 20% РѕС‚ Р±Р°Р·РѕРІРѕР№ Р°СЂРµРЅРґС‹ СЃРєР»Р°РґР° (РґР»СЏ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃР°)
      const maintenance = Math.round(def.rentalIncome * 0.2);
      
      const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
        balance: balanceDecimal,
        amount: String(maintenance),
        direction: 'expense',
        monthIndex: month,
        accountId: 'player_main',
        category: 'warehouse_maintenance',
        description: `Р–РљРҐ СЃРєР»Р°РґР°: ${def.name}`,
        relatedEntityId: whId,
      });
      if (tx.ok) {
        balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
        journal.push(tx.entry);
      }
    }

    return {
      ...state,
      rentedProperties: nextRented,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 12): РјРµСЃСЏС‡РЅС‹Р№ РїР»Р°С‚С‘Р¶ РїРѕ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Р°Рј, РїСЂРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РєРё Рё РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ
 * РєСЂРµРґРёС‚РЅРѕРіРѕ СЂРµР№С‚РёРЅРіР° (finance/creditEngine.ts).
 */
const creditsProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'credits',
  order: 30,
  run: (state, month) => {
    const activeLoan = (state.loans ?? []).find((l) => l.status === 'active');

    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    let creditScore = state.creditScore ?? 100;
    let loans = state.loans ?? [];
    let businesses = state.businesses ?? [];
    let warehouses = state.warehouses ?? [];
    let fixedDeposits = state.fixedDeposits ?? [];
    let marketPositions = state.marketPositions ?? [];
    let gameOver = state.gameOver ?? false;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];

    // в”Ђв”Ђ 1. РџР»Р°С‚С‘Р¶ РїРѕ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅРѕРјСѓ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Сѓ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    if (activeLoan) {
      // availableIncome = balanceDecimal (СѓР¶Рµ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёСЏ РґРѕС…РѕРґРѕРІ)
      const available = balanceDecimal;

      const result = settleLoanMonth(activeLoan, available, creditScore);

      // РЎРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ РїР»Р°С‚С‘Р¶ (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё payment > 0)
      if (Number(result.payment) > 0) {
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: result.payment,
          direction: 'expense',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'loan_principal',
          description: `РџР»Р°С‚С‘Р¶ РїРѕ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚Сѓ`,
          relatedEntityId: activeLoan.id,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
      }

      // РЁС‚СЂР°С„ (РµСЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р»)
      if (Number(result.penalty) > 0) {
        const penalty = result.penalty;
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: penalty,
          direction: 'expense',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'loan_interest',
          description: `РЁС‚СЂР°С„ Р·Р° РїСЂРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РєСѓ`,
          relatedEntityId: activeLoan.id,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
      }

      creditScore = result.creditScore;

      // в”Ђв”Ђ Р”РµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ Р±Р°РЅРєР° в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
      if (result.action === 'seize_business' && businesses.length > 0) {
        // РџСЂРѕРґР°С‚СЊ СЃР°РјС‹Р№ РґРµС€С‘РІС‹Р№ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ Р·Р° 50%
        const sold = [...businesses].sort((a, b) => {
          const aVal = Number(a.cashBalance ?? 0);
          const bVal = Number(b.cashBalance ?? 0);
          return aVal - bVal;
        })[0];

        if (sold) {
          const soldValue = Number(sold.cashBalance ?? 0) * 0.5;
          businesses = businesses.filter((b) => b.id !== sold.id);

          // РџРѕРіР°СЃРёС‚СЊ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РґРѕР»РіР°
          const newLoanBalance = Math.max(
            0,
            Number(result.loan.balance) - soldValue,
          );
          result.loan = { ...result.loan, balance: String(newLoanBalance) };

          journal.push({
            id: `ledger-seize-${sold.id}-${month}`,
            monthIndex: month,
            accountId: `business:${sold.id}`,
            direction: 'expense',
            category: 'loan_interest',
            amount: String(soldValue),
            description: `Р‘Р°РЅРє Р·Р°Р±СЂР°Р»: ${sold.name} (50% СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё)`,
            relatedEntityId: sold.id,
          });
        }
      }

      if (result.action === 'bankruptcy' && !gameOver) {
        // в”Ђв”Ђ РџСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р»РёРєРІРёРґР°С†РёСЏ Р°РєС‚РёРІРѕРІ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
        // РЎРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РїСЂРѕРґР°С‘Рј РІСЃС‘, С‡С‚Рѕ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ, РїРѕС‚РѕРј СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј, С…РІР°С‚РёР»Рѕ Р»Рё РЅР° РґРѕР»Рі.
        let recovered = 0;

        // 1. Р‘РёР·РЅРµСЃС‹: РїРѕРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ (owned) + РѕР±РѕСЂСѓРґРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ + РєР°СЃСЃР°
        for (const biz of businesses) {
          const bizDef = BUSINESSES.find((d) => d.id === biz.defId);
          if (!bizDef) continue;
          const premisesValue =
            (bizDef.premisesPrice ?? bizDef.fixedCosts * 24) * 0.5;
          const equipmentValue =
            (bizDef.startingEquipment ?? bizDef.fixedCosts * 2) * 0.3;
          const cash = Number(biz.cashBalance ?? 0);
          recovered += premisesValue + equipmentValue + cash;
        }
        businesses = [];

        // 2. РўРѕРІР°СЂС‹ РЅР° СЃРєР»Р°РґР°С… вЂ” СЂР°СЃРїСЂРѕРґР°Р¶Р° РїРѕ Г—0.4
        for (const wh of warehouses) {
          for (const item of Object.values(wh.items)) {
            const res = RESOURCES_BY_ID[item.resourceId];
            if (!res) continue;
            recovered += Number(res.basePrice) * 0.4 * item.quantity;
          }
        }
        warehouses = warehouses.map((w) => ({ ...w, items: {} }));

        // 3. Р’РєР»Р°РґС‹ вЂ” Р·Р°Р±РёСЂР°РµРј С‚РµР»Рѕ (РїСЂРѕС†РµРЅС‚С‹ РЅРµ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»СЏРµРј)
        for (const dep of fixedDeposits) {
          recovered += Number(dep.amount);
        }
        fixedDeposits = [];

        // 4. РђРєС†РёРё вЂ” РїРѕ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ СЂС‹РЅРѕС‡РЅРѕР№ С†РµРЅРµ
        for (const pos of marketPositions) {
          const company = state.marketCompanies?.find((c) => c.id === pos.companyId);
          if (!company) continue;
          recovered += Number(company.price) * pos.shares;
        }
        marketPositions = [];

        // в”Ђв”Ђ РџРѕРіР°С€РµРЅРёРµ РґРѕР»РіР° в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
        balanceDecimal = String(Number(balanceDecimal) + recovered);
        const debtLeft = Number(result.loan.balance);
        const finalBalance = Number(balanceDecimal) - debtLeft;

        if (finalBalance >= 0) {
          // Р”РѕР»Рі РїРѕРєСЂС‹С‚ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶Р°РµРј Р±РµР· gameOver
          balanceDecimal = String(finalBalance);
          result.loan = { ...result.loan, balance: '0', status: 'paid' };
          journal.push({
            id: `ledger-liquidate-${month}`,
            monthIndex: month,
            accountId: 'player_main',
            direction: 'expense',
            category: 'loan_interest',
            amount: String(Math.round(debtLeft)),
            description: `Р›РёРєРІРёРґР°С†РёСЏ Р°РєС‚РёРІРѕРІ: РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РµРЅРѕ ${Math.round(recovered)} в‚Ѕ, РґРѕР»Рі ${Math.round(debtLeft)} в‚Ѕ РїРѕРєСЂС‹С‚`,
          });
        } else {
          // Р”РѕР»Рі РЅРµ РїРѕРєСЂС‹С‚ вЂ” gameOver
          balanceDecimal = '0';
          result.loan = { ...result.loan, status: 'defaulted' };
          gameOver = true;
          journal.push({
            id: `ledger-gameover-${month}`,
            monthIndex: month,
            accountId: 'player_main',
            direction: 'expense',
            category: 'loan_interest',
            amount: String(Math.round(debtLeft)),
            description: `Р‘Р°РЅРєСЂРѕС‚СЃС‚РІРѕ: Р»РёРєРІРёРґР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕРєСЂС‹Р»Р° ${Math.round(recovered)} в‚Ѕ РёР· ${Math.round(debtLeft)} в‚Ѕ РґРѕР»РіР°. РРіСЂР° РѕРєРѕРЅС‡РµРЅР°.`,
          });
        }
      }
      // РћР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ РєСЂРµРґРёС‚ РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІРµ
      loans = loans.map((l) => (l.id === activeLoan.id ? result.loan : l));
    }

    // в”Ђв”Ђ 2. Р РѕСЃС‚ СЂРµР№С‚РёРЅРіР° Р·Р° Р°РєС‚РёРІС‹/СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ/РІРѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    const activeLoanAfter = loans.find((l) => l.status === 'active');
    const totalBusinessLevels = businesses.reduce(
      (sum, b) => sum + (b.level ?? 1),
      0,
    );
    const companyLevel = Math.max(1, Math.floor(totalBusinessLevels / 5) + 1);
    const companyAgeMonths = month;

    creditScore = monthlyCreditScoreGrowth({
      score: creditScore,
      companyLevel,
      companyAgeMonths,
      debtFree: !activeLoanAfter,
      positiveAssets: Number(balanceDecimal) > 0,
    });

    return {
      ...state,
      loans,
      businesses,
      warehouses,
      fixedDeposits,
      marketPositions,
      gameOver,
      creditScore,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 9): РґРёРІРёРґРµРЅРґС‹ РїРѕ Р°РєС†РёСЏРј, РєСѓРїРѕРЅС‹ РїРѕ РѕР±Р»РёРіР°С†РёСЏРј, РґРѕС…РѕРґ РїРѕ С„РѕРЅРґР°Рј
 * (finance/investmentEngine.ts, market/marketEngine.ts).
 */
const investmentProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'investment',
  order: 35,
  run: (state, month) => {
    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];

    // в”Ђв”Ђ Р¤РѕРЅРґС‹: РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёРµ РґРѕС…РѕРґР° в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    let funds = state.funds ?? [];
    funds = funds.map((position) => {
      const def = FUND_DEFINITIONS_BY_ID[position.fundId];
      if (!def) return position;

      const result = accrueFundMonth(position, def);
      const income = result.income;
      if (Number(income) > 0) {
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: income,
          direction: 'income',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'fund_income',
          description: `Р¤РѕРЅРґ: ${def.name}`,
          relatedEntityId: position.fundId,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
      }
      return result.position;
    });

    // в”Ђв”Ђ РћР±Р»РёРіР°С†РёРё: РєСѓРїРѕРЅС‹ + СЃРѕР·СЂРµРІР°РЅРёРµ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    let bonds = state.bonds ?? [];
    const maturedBonds: BondPosition[] = [];
    bonds = bonds.map((position) => {
      const def = BOND_DEFINITIONS_BY_ID[position.bondId];
      if (!def) return position;

      const result = accrueBondMonth(
        position,
        def.annualCouponRate,
        def.faceValue,
      );

      if (Number(result.coupon) > 0) {
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: result.coupon,
          direction: 'income',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'coupon',
          description: `РљСѓРїРѕРЅ: ${def.name}`,
          relatedEntityId: position.bondId,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
      }

      if (result.matured) {
        const faceValue = String(Number(def.faceValue) * position.quantity);
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: faceValue,
          direction: 'income',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'bond_maturity',
          description: `РџРѕРіР°С€РµРЅРёРµ: ${def.name}`,
          relatedEntityId: position.bondId,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
        maturedBonds.push(position);
        return result.position;
      }
      return result.position;
    });

    if (maturedBonds.length > 0) {
      bonds = bonds.filter(
        (b) => !maturedBonds.some((m) => m.bondId === b.bondId),
      );
    }

    // в”Ђв”Ђ РњРµС‚Р°Р»Р»С‹: РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ С†РµРЅС‹ в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    const metals = (state.metals ?? []).map((m) => ({
      ...m,
      currentPrice: tickMetalPrice(m.metalId, m.currentPrice),
    }));

    // в”Ђв”Ђ Р’РљР›РђР”Р« (order 35, РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё investmentProcessor) в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
    // 1. РќР°РєРѕРїРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‡С‘С‚: +2% РІ РјРµСЃСЏС† РѕС‚ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РєР°.
    let savingsBalance = state.savingsBalance ?? '0';
    if (Number(savingsBalance) > 0) {
      const interest = Number(savingsBalance) * 0.02;
      savingsBalance = String(Number(savingsBalance) + interest);
      journal.push({
        id: `ledger-savings-interest-${month}-${Date.now()}`,
        monthIndex: month,
        accountId: 'player_main',
        direction: 'income',
        category: 'savings_interest',
        amount: String(Math.round(interest)),
        description: 'РџСЂРѕС†РµРЅС‚С‹ РїРѕ РЅР°РєРѕРїРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕРјСѓ СЃС‡С‘С‚Сѓ',
      });
    }

    // 2. РЎСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Рµ РІРєР»Р°РґС‹: monthsLeft -= 1. РќР° 0 вЂ” Р·Р°С‡РёСЃР»СЏРµРј С‚РµР»Рѕ + %.
    const remainingDeposits: typeof state.fixedDeposits = [];
    for (const dep of state.fixedDeposits ?? []) {
      const monthsLeft = dep.monthsLeft - 1;
      if (monthsLeft <= 0) {
        const payout = Number(dep.amount) * Math.pow(1 + dep.monthlyRate, dep.termMonths);
        const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
          balance: balanceDecimal,
          amount: String(Math.round(payout)),
          direction: 'income',
          monthIndex: month,
          accountId: 'player_main',
          category: 'deposit_maturity',
          description: 'Р’РєР»Р°Рґ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚ (СЃСЂРѕРє РёСЃС‚С‘Рє)',
          relatedEntityId: dep.id,
        });
        if (tx.ok) {
          balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
          journal.push(tx.entry);
        }
      } else {
        remainingDeposits.push({ ...dep, monthsLeft });
      }
    }

    return {
      ...state,
      funds,
      bonds,
      metals,
      savingsBalance,
      fixedDeposits: remainingDeposits,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};

const marketProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'market',
  order: 40,
  run: (state, month) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(state.marketCompanies) || state.marketCompanies.length === 0) {
      return state;
    }

    // 1. Р”РёРІРёРґРµРЅРґС‹
    let balanceDecimal = state.balanceDecimal;
    const journal = [...state.financeJournal];
    let totalDividends = '0';

    for (const pos of state.marketPositions ?? []) {
      const company = state.marketCompanies.find((c) => c.id === pos.companyId);
      if (!company) continue;
      if (company.annualDividendYield <= 0) continue;

      const perShare = calculateMonthlyDividendPerShare(company);
      const total = moneyMul(perShare, pos.shares);
      if (Number(total) <= 0) continue;

      const tx = applyWalletTransaction({
        balance: balanceDecimal,
        amount: total,
        direction: 'income',
        monthIndex: month,
        accountId: 'player_main',
        category: 'dividend',
        description: `Р”РёРІРёРґРµРЅРґС‹: ${company.name} Г— ${pos.shares}`,
        relatedEntityId: company.id,
      });
      if (tx.ok) {
        balanceDecimal = tx.balance;
        journal.push(tx.entry);
        totalDividends = moneyAdd(totalDividends, total);
      }
    }

    // 2. Р РµРґРєРёРµ С€РѕРєРё РґР»СЏ СЃРїРµРєСѓР»СЏС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… РєРѕРјРїР°РЅРёР№
    const shockedCompanies = state.marketCompanies.map((c) =>
      applySpeculativeMonthShock(c),
    );

    // РЈРІРµРґРѕРјР»РµРЅРёРµ
    if (Number(totalDividends) > 0) {
      // РџСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕРµ СѓРІРµРґРѕРјР»РµРЅРёРµ Р±РµР· toast вЂ” toast РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ store С‡РµСЂРµР· onCompletedMonths.
      // Р—РґРµСЃСЊ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ Р»РѕРіРёСЂСѓРµРј РІ Р¶СѓСЂРЅР°Р».
    }

    return {
      ...state,
      marketCompanies: shockedCompanies,
      balanceDecimal,
      balance: moneyToNumber(balanceDecimal),
      financeJournal: journal,
    };
  },
};

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 8): СЂРѕР»Р» РєСЂСѓРїРЅС‹С… СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° Рё РёСЃС‚РµС‡РµРЅРёРµ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёР№
 * (events/eventEngine.ts).
 */
const eventsProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'events',
  order: 8, // РґРѕ businessesProcessor (15), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СЃРїСЂРѕСЃ СѓСЃРїРµР» РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРёС‚СЊСЃСЏ
  run: (state, month) => {
    const { activeEvents, createdEvent } = rollMonthlyEvent(
      DOMAIN_EVENT_TEMPLATES,
      state.activeEvents ?? [],
      month,
    );

    let news = state.news ?? [];

    if (createdEvent) {
      news = [
        {
          id: `news_event_${createdEvent.id}`,
          monthIndex: month,
          title: `рџ“ў ${createdEvent.title}`,
          body: createdEvent.description,
          eventId: createdEvent.id,
          category: 'event',
        },
        ...news,
      ];
    }

    // РћС‡РёСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ РЅРѕРІРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃС‚Р°СЂС€Рµ 12 РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ
    const minMonth = month - 12;
    news = news.filter((n) => n.monthIndex >= minMonth).slice(0, 200);

    return {
      ...state,
      activeEvents,
      news,
    };
  },
};

/**
 * TODO(Р‘Р»РѕРє 3): СЃРЅРёРјРѕРє С‡РёСЃС‚РѕРіРѕ РєР°РїРёС‚Р°Р»Р° РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°. РџРѕРєР° РіСЂР°С„РёРє
 * СЃРѕР±РёСЂР°РµС‚СЃСЏ С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃС‚Р°СЂСѓСЋ capitalHistory РІ store вЂ” РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј.
 */
const contractsProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'contracts',
  order: 45,
  run: (state, month) => {
    let contracts = expireContracts(state.contracts ?? [], month);
    let news = state.news ?? [];

    // Р“РµРЅРµСЂРёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ, РµСЃР»Рё РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅС‹С… РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ 3
    const availableCount = contracts.filter((c) => c.status === 'available').length;
    if (availableCount < 3) {
      const fresh = generateContracts(month, 2);
      contracts = [...fresh, ...contracts].slice(0, 30);

      for (const nc of fresh) {
        news = [
          {
            id: `news_contract_new_${nc.id}`,
            monthIndex: month,
            title: `рџ“‹ РќРѕРІС‹Р№ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚: ${nc.title}`,
            body: nc.description,
            companyId: nc.clientCompanyId,
            category: 'contract',
          },
          ...news,
        ];
      }
    }

    return {
      ...state,
      contracts,
      news: news.slice(0, 200),
    };
  },
};
const capitalSnapshotProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'capital-snapshot',
  order: 90,
  run: (state) => {
    // РЎРЅРёРјРѕРє РґРµР»Р°РµРј С‡РµСЂРµР· store вЂ” РѕРЅ СѓРјРµРµС‚ РІС‹С‡РёСЃР»СЏС‚СЊ РєР°РїРёС‚Р°Р».
    // Р—РґРµСЃСЊ РЅРµ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј captureCapitalSnapshot РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ, РїРѕС‚РѕРјСѓ С‡С‚Рѕ
    // processor СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ СЃ С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Рј state. Р’РјРµСЃС‚Рѕ СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј state
    // СЃ С„Р»Р°РіРѕРј вЂ” РЅРѕ РїСЂРѕС‰Рµ РІС‹Р·РІР°С‚СЊ store РїРѕСЃР»Рµ processCompletedMonths.
    // РџРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СЃРЅРёРјРѕРє РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ onCompletedMonths РџРћРЎР›Р• runMonthlyProcessors.
    return state;
  },
};
/**
 * РЎР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє РїСЂРѕРґР°Р¶ NPC РІ РЅР°С‡Р°Р»Рµ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РјРµСЃСЏС†Р°.
 * order: 1 вЂ” СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РІСЃРµС… РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРѕСЂРѕРІ.
 */
const npcDemandResetProcessor: MonthlyProcessor<GameState> = {
  id: 'npc-demand-reset',
  order: 1,
  run: (state) => ({ ...state, monthlyNpcSold: {} }),
};
/**
 * Р•РґРёРЅС‹Р№ СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРѕСЂРѕРІ. РџСЂРё РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРё РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ вЂ” РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РґРѕРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµРј
 * РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ СЃ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґСЏС‰РёРј order. РџРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РЅРµ Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёС‚ РѕС‚ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєР°
 * РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІРµ: processCompletedMonths СЃРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚ РїРѕ order.
 */
export const MONTHLY_PROCESSORS: MonthlyProcessor<GameState>[] = [
  npcDemandResetProcessor,
  eventsProcessor,
  payrollProcessor,
  competitorProcessor,
  enterpriseProcessor,
  businessesProcessor,
  warehouseProcessor,
  cityTreasuryProcessor,
  rentalProcessor,
  creditsProcessor,
  investmentProcessor,
  marketProcessor,
  contractsProcessor,
  capitalSnapshotProcessor,
];

/**
 * РџСЂРѕРіРЅР°С‚СЊ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РёРіСЂС‹ С‡РµСЂРµР· РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃРѕСЂС‹ РґР»СЏ РІСЃРµС… Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅРЅС‹С… РјРµСЃСЏС†РµРІ.
 * Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ state (РЅРµ РјСѓС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚ РёСЃС…РѕРґРЅС‹Р№).
 */
export function runMonthlyProcessors(
  state: GameState,
  completedMonths: number[],
): GameState {
  return processCompletedMonths(state, completedMonths, MONTHLY_PROCESSORS);
}

export interface FundDefinition {
  id: Id;
  name: string;

  /**
   * Р“РѕРґРѕРІР°СЏ РѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјР°СЏ РґРѕС…РѕРґРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РІ РґРѕР»СЏС…:
   * 0.08 РѕР·РЅР°С‡Р°РµС‚ 8% РіРѕРґРѕРІС‹С….
   */
  annualYield: number;

  /**
   * РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјРѕРµ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№РЅРѕРµ РєРѕР»РµР±Р°РЅРёРµ Р·Р° РјРµСЃСЏС†.
   * РќР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ 0.002 вЂ” РїСЂРёРјРµСЂРЅРѕ 0.2%.
   */

----- interface BusinessRuntime -----
export interface BusinessRuntime {
  id: Id;
  /** РЎСЃС‹Р»РєР° РЅР° BusinessDef РёР· data/businesses.ts */
  defId?: Id;
  name: string;
  sectorId: string;
    /** ID СЃРєР»Р°РґР°, Рє РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРјСѓ РїСЂРёРІСЏР·Р°РЅ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ. Р•СЃР»Рё undefined вЂ” СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ. */
  linkedWarehouseId?: string;
    /** Р›РѕРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РёРЅРІРµРЅС‚Р°СЂСЊ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃР° (РґР»СЏ СЂРѕР·РЅРёС‡РЅС‹С… Р·Р°РєСѓРїРѕРє РЅР°РїСЂСЏРјСѓСЋ). */
  inventory?: Record<string, { quantity: number; averageUnitCost: Money }>;
    /** РњРµСЃСЏС†, РІ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РёР» СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚РѕРІСѓСЋ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРєСѓ (РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ РјРµСЃСЏС† Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ). */
  initialSuppliedMonth?: number;
  level: number;

  status: BusinessStatus;

  /**
   * РћР±РѕСЂРѕС‚РЅС‹Рµ СЃСЂРµРґСЃС‚РІР° Р±РёР·РЅРµСЃР°.
   */
  cashBalance: Money;

  /**
   * Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІР°СЏ РІС‹СЂСѓС‡РєР° Р·Р° РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕР№ РјРµСЃСЏС† РґРѕ РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»РµР№.
   */
  baseMonthlyRevenue: Money;

  demandMultiplier: number;
  productivityMultiplier: number;

  monthlyPayroll: Money;
  monthlyRent: Money;
  monthlyUtilities: Money;
  monthlyMaintenance: Money;

  taxRate: number;

  resourceNeeds: ResourceRequirement[];

  monthsWithoutSupply: number;
  monthsStopped: number;


----- interface EmployeeRuntime -----
export interface EmployeeRuntime {
  id: Id;
  businessId: Id;
  role: EmployeeRole;
  name: string;

  qualification: number;
  monthlySalary: Money;

  monthsEmployed: number;
  monthsRemaining: number;
}

export interface CareerProgress {
  careerId: string;
  stageIndex: number;
  experience: number;
  lastWorkedMonth: number;
}


export interface EventEffect {
  kind:
    | 'sectorDemand'
    | 'sectorCost'
    | 'resourcePrice'
    | 'companyPrice';

  targetId: Id;
  multiplier: number;
}

export interface EventTemplate {
  id: Id;
  title: string;
  description: string;

  weight: number;
  durationMonths: number;

  effects: EventEffect[];
